{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NPKdKa4hzBqo1eHhQHs7fJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { MigrationInterface, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n2\t\n3\t/**\n4\t * MSG-HIGH-060: drop the per-channel `aiServiceUrl` exfiltration vector.\n5\t *\n6\t * `channels` is a PER-TENANT cloned table (it is in the `messaging` module's\n7\t * tenant `tables` set — schema-manager.service.ts). The migration runner pins\n8\t * search_path to `messaging` (source template) and then to each `tenant_<uuid>`\n9\t * schema, so the table name MUST be UNQUALIFIED (resolved against\n10\t * current_schema()) or the DROP only ever touches the source template and every\n11\t * provisioned tenant keeps the column (and its stored endpoints) forever.\n12\t * Mirrors the farm precedent 1801100000000-EncryptFarmWorkerPii.\n13\t *\n14\t * The column let any channel member point the AI at an arbitrary public HTTPS\n15\t * endpoint they controlled; the bridge then POSTed tenantId + the last 50\n16\t * conversation messages there. SSRF validation only blocked internal targets,\n17\t * so it did nothing against exfiltration to an attacker's public server. BYOK\n18\t * (Faz 1) routes all AI through ai-service over NATS with the tenant's own key,\n19\t * so the override is obsolete AND unsafe.\n20\t *\n21\t * Safe drop: the column is nullable and, as of this release, unread by the\n22\t * service (the entity field and the HTTP-forward path are removed in the same\n23\t * change). down() restores the nullable column shape only — prior values are\n24\t * intentionally not recovered (they were security-relevant and are gone).\n25\t */\n26\tconst TABLE = 'channels';\n27\t\n28\texport class DropChannelAiServiceUrl1802000000000 implements MigrationInterface {\n29\t  public async up(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n30\t    await queryRunner.query(\n31\t      `ALTER TABLE \"${TABLE}\" DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS \"aiServiceUrl\"`,\n32\t    );\n33\t  }\n34\t\n35\t  public async down(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n36\t    await queryRunner.query(\n37\t      `ALTER TABLE \"${TABLE}\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"aiServiceUrl\" character varying(512)`,\n38\t    );\n39\t  }\n40\t}\n41\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ff92a1d8-3185-463a-9cf3-02f1306689af","uuid":"d14d0819-8c4e-40f7-8718-d20d8246962b","timestamp":"2026-09-21T02:15:10.270Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-d333052f1946/apps/messaging-service/src/migrations/1802000000000-DropChannelAiServiceUrl.ts","content":"import { MigrationInterface, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n\n/**\n * MSG-HIGH-060: drop the per-channel `aiServiceUrl` exfiltration vector.\n *\n * `channels` is a PER-TENANT cloned table (it is in the `messaging` module's\n * tenant `tables` set — schema-manager.service.ts). The migration runner pins\n * search_path to `messaging` (source template) and then to each `tenant_<uuid>`\n * schema, so the table name MUST be UNQUALIFIED (resolved against\n * current_schema()) or the DROP only ever touches the source template and every\n * provisioned tenant keeps the column (and its stored endpoints) forever.\n * Mirrors the farm precedent 1801100000000-EncryptFarmWorkerPii.\n *\n * The column let any channel member point the AI at an arbitrary public HTTPS\n * endpoint they controlled; the bridge then POSTed tenantId + the last 50\n * conversation messages there. SSRF validation only blocked internal targets,\n * so it did nothing against exfiltration to an attacker's public server. BYOK\n * (Faz 1) routes all AI through ai-service over NATS with the tenant's own key,\n * so the override is obsolete AND unsafe.\n *\n * Safe drop: the column is nullable and, as of this release, unread by the\n * service (the entity field and the HTTP-forward path are removed in the same\n * change). down() restores the nullable column shape only — prior values are\n * intentionally not recovered (they were security-relevant and are gone).\n */\nconst TABLE = 'channels';\n\nexport class DropChannelAiServiceUrl1802000000000 implements MigrationInterface {\n  public async up(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n    await queryRunner.query(\n      `ALTER TABLE \"${TABLE}\" DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS \"aiServiceUrl\"`,\n    );\n  }\n\n  public async down(queryRunner: QueryRunner): Promise<void> {\n    await queryRunner.query(\n      `ALTER TABLE \"${TABLE}\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"aiServiceUrl\" character varying(512)`,\n    );\n  }\n}\n","numLines":41,"startLine":1,"totalLines":41}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EvT7CoVZxGxveSUwG6YChS","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { readFileSync } from 'fs';\n2\timport { basename, join, relative } from 'path';\n3\timport { execFileSync } from 'child_process';\n4\t\n5\timport { MODULE_SCHEMAS } from '../../libs/backend-common/src/database/schema-manager.service';\n6\timport { stripComments } from './helpers/ts-source';\n7\t\n8\t/**\n9\t * Tenant provisioning is migration REPLAY: the orchestrator pins\n10\t * `search_path` to `tenant_<uuid>` and runs each tenant-aware service's\n11\t * migration set. That mechanism only redirects UNQUALIFIED DDL. Anything that\n12\t * names its schema explicitly — or re-pins the session to the source schema —\n13\t * escapes it, lands in the source schema, and is STILL recorded in the tenant's\n14\t * ledger as applied. This spec is the gate for that class.\n15\t *\n16\t * ## Why this spec was rewritten (DATA-HIGH-012)\n17\t *\n18\t * The previous detector was a single regex looking for a DDL keyword followed\n19\t * by a `\"<source schema>\".` identifier, plus a filename allowlist and a blanket\n20\t * exclusion for every Baseline. It enumerated one SPELLING of the defect rather\n21\t * than testing the property, and both halves of that leaked:\n22\t *\n23\t *   - MSG-HIGH-077 shipped a per-tenant migration that re-pinned its own source\n24\t *     schema — functionally identical to qualifying every statement in the file\n25\t *     — CITING THIS GUARD in its docblock as evidence of its own correctness.\n26\t *   - DATA-CRITICAL-010 (no new tenant can be provisioned) lived entirely\n27\t *     inside the Baseline exclusion.\n28\t *\n29\t * It now asks the actual question — *does this migration issue DDL against a\n30\t * PER-TENANT table in the source schema, by either spelling?* — using\n31\t * `MODULE_SCHEMAS` as the classifier. That is the same SSoT the entity layer\n32\t * obeys via `entity-schema-declaration`, so the guard cannot drift from it, and\n33\t * the Baselines are now checked like every other migration rather than excused.\n34\t *\n35\t * `scripts/migration/dequalify-tenant-baselines.mjs` reads the same registry to\n36\t * decide what to rewrite. Two consumers of one SSoT, not two SSoTs.\n37\t *\n38\t * ## Fail-closed\n39\t *\n40\t * A qualified identifier the registry cannot explain is a VIOLATION, not a\n41\t * pass. An unregistered per-tenant table is exactly the shape that would\n42\t * otherwise slip through, so \"I don't recognise this\" resolves against the\n43\t * migration and lands in the reviewer allowlist below with a written reason.\n44\t */\n45\t\n46\tconst REPO_ROOT = join(__dirname, '..', '..');\n47\t\n48\t/** Source schema per tenant-aware migration directory. */\n49\tconst TENANT_AWARE_MIGRATION_DIRS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, string]> = [\n50\t  ['apps/farm-service/src/database/migrations', 'farm'],\n51\t  ['apps/sensor-service/src/database/migrations', 'sensor'],\n52\t  ['apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations', 'hr'],\n53\t  ['apps/messaging-service/src/migrations', 'messaging'],\n54\t  ['apps/messaging-service/src/database/migrations', 'messaging'],\n55\t  ['apps/alert-engine/src/database/migrations', 'alert'],\n56\t  ['apps/ai-service/src/database/migrations', 'ai'],\n57\t  ['apps/hydroponics-service/src/database/migrations', 'hydroponics'],\n58\t];\n59\t\n60\t/** A DDL verb + object immediately preceding a qualified identifier. */\n61\tconst DDL_LEAD =\n62\t  /\\b(?:CREATE|ALTER|DROP|TRUNCATE)\\s+(?:UNIQUE\\s+)?(?:TABLE|INDEX|TYPE|SEQUENCE|VIEW|MATERIALIZED\\s+VIEW)\\b(?:\\s+IF\\s+(?:NOT\\s+)?EXISTS)?[\\s\\S]{0,220}$/i;\n63\t\n64\t/**\n65\t * Tables that exist in a shipped migration's DDL but in NEITHER\n66\t * `MODULE_SCHEMAS[].tables` nor `[].infrastructureTables`, because a later\n67\t * migration retired them. They cannot be classified and they cannot be edited\n68\t * out of an immutable migration, so they are named here with the migration that\n69\t * retired them. A NEW name appearing in a failure means an entity shipped\n70\t * without its registry entry — register it, do not add it here.\n71\t */\n72\tconst RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlySet<string>> = new Map([\n73\t  // Superseded by the `sensor_metrics` hypertable; the per-tenant copy of that\n74\t  // is created unqualified by 1815000000000.\n75\t  ['sensor', new Set(['sensor_readings'])],\n76\t  // Dropped by 1802100000000-DropTenantAiSettings.\n77\t  ['messaging', new Set(['tenant_ai_settings'])],\n78\t]);\n79\t\n80\t/**\n81\t * Migrations whose source-schema-qualified DDL has been REVIEWED as legitimate.\n82\t * This is a reviewer-gated allowlist in THIS spec, not a self-service docblock\n83\t * marker: ORPHAN-HIGH-408's `CreateAiProposedActions` used the old\n84\t * `TENANT_AWARE_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL_OK` docblock marker to schema-qualify a\n85\t * PER-TENANT table, which the replay then never landed in any tenant. A\n86\t * self-service marker bypasses CI silently; an allowlist edit shows up in the\n87\t * PR diff. Adding an entry must be justified in writing.\n88\t */\n89\tconst REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([\n90\t  // Source-only enum reclaim/heal of the 2026-07-07 QualityGrade outage.\n91\t  '1804400000000-DropOrphanQualityGradeEnum.ts',\n92\t  '1804500000000-HealBehindTenantQualityGrade.ts',\n93\t  // Cross-tenant source-schema tables + partition-contract index (not cloned).\n94\t  '1800300000000-SensorV2TenantFkAndLicenseGrant.ts',\n95\t  '1802000000000-AddVfdDeviceModelSeriesPumpTags.ts',\n96\t  '1804000000000-ConsolidateVfdRegisterMappingsToSensorSchema.ts',\n97\t  // SENSOR-CRITICAL-007 Slice 1: nullable edge-binding columns on the per-tenant\n98\t  // vfd_devices table via the SAME source-template + tenant fan-out pattern as\n99\t  // 1802000000000 above (canonical `sensor` copy + every `tenant_*` copy). Not a\n100\t  // per-tenant table wrongly schema-qualified — the fan-out lands it everywhere.\n101\t  '1808000000000-AddVfdDeviceEdgeBinding.ts',\n102\t  // SEC-MEDIUM-083: partial unique index on the per-tenant vfd_change_sets\n103\t  // table — canonical `sensor` copy + tenant fan-out (same pattern as\n104\t  // 1808000000000 above). Per-tenant, not misqualified.\n105\t  '1818000000000-EnforceSingleActiveVfdChangeSet.ts',\n106\t  // SENSOR-HIGH-064: nullable config-ack tracking columns on the per-tenant\n107\t  // edge_devices table via the SAME source-template + tenant fan-out pattern\n108\t  // (canonical `sensor` copy + every `tenant_*` copy).\n109\t  '1809000000000-AddEdgeDeviceConfigAckTracking.ts',\n110\t  // SENSOR-HIGH-083: per-tenant calibration_events table + nullable\n111\t  // calibration_interval_days column on sensor_data_channels, both created in the\n112\t  // canonical `sensor` source schema and fanned out into every `tenant_*` schema\n113\t  // (CREATE TABLE … LIKE INCLUDING ALL for the clone). Per-tenant, not misqualified.\n114\t  '1810000000000-AddCalibrationEventsAndInterval.ts',\n115\t  '1803000000000-HashProvisioningSecretsAtRest.ts',\n116\t  '1800500000000-EnsureMessagingPartitionContract.ts',\n117\t  // ORPHAN-HIGH-408: immutable historical BUG — `ai_proposed_actions` is a\n118\t  // per-tenant table wrongly schema-qualified. Retained only because migrations\n119\t  // are immutable; HEALED by 1803100000000-HealAiProposedActionsUnqualified.\n120\t  // DO NOT copy this pattern: per-tenant tables must be UNQUALIFIED.\n121\t  '1803000000000-CreateAiProposedActions.ts',\n122\t]);\n123\t\n124\tinterface Registry {\n125\t  readonly perTenant: ReadonlySet<string>;\n126\t  readonly infrastructure: ReadonlySet<string>;\n127\t  readonly retired: ReadonlySet<string>;\n128\t}\n129\t\n130\tfunction registryFor(schema: string): Registry {\n131\t  const entry = MODULE_SCHEMAS.find((m) => m.sourceSchema === schema);\n132\t  if (entry === undefined) {\n133\t    throw new Error(`MODULE_SCHEMAS has no entry for source schema \"${schema}\"`);\n134\t  }\n135\t  return {\n136\t    perTenant: new Set([...entry.tables, ...(entry.referenceDataTables ?? [])]),\n137\t    infrastructure: new Set(entry.infrastructureTables ?? []),\n138\t    retired: RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES.get(schema) ?? new Set<string>(),\n139\t  };\n140\t}\n141\t\n142\t/**\n143\t * `CREATE [UNIQUE] INDEX \"name\" ON \"schema\".\"table\"` — an index inherits its\n144\t * table's classification, and `DROP INDEX \"schema\".\"name\"` names no table, so\n145\t * the map has to be built from the create side first.\n146\t */\n147\tfunction indexOwners(source: string, schema: string): Map<string, string> {\n148\t  const owners = new Map<string, string>();\n149\t  const rx = new RegExp(\n150\t    `CREATE\\\\s+(?:UNIQUE\\\\s+)?INDEX\\\\s+(?:IF\\\\s+NOT\\\\s+EXISTS\\\\s+)?\"([^\"]+)\"\\\\s+ON\\\\s+(?:\"${schema}\"\\\\.)?\"([^\"]+)\"`,\n151\t    'gi',\n152\t  );\n153\t  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n154\t    if (match[1] !== undefined && match[2] !== undefined) owners.set(match[1], match[2]);\n155\t  }\n156\t  return owners;\n157\t}\n158\t\n159\t/**\n160\t * Enum type names from their own definitions rather than from a naming\n161\t * convention — farm's `equipment_category` is an enum and carries no `_enum`\n162\t * suffix. Enums are DELIBERATELY left qualified: existing tenants SHARE the\n163\t * source-schema type (`CREATE TABLE … LIKE INCLUDING ALL` shares a type, it does\n164\t * not clone it), so de-qualifying would split the fleet in two.\n165\t */\n166\tfunction enumTypes(source: string, schema: string): Set<string> {\n167\t  const names = new Set<string>();\n168\t  const rx = new RegExp(`CREATE\\\\s+TYPE\\\\s+\"${schema}\"\\\\.\"([^\"]+)\"\\\\s+AS\\\\s+ENUM`, 'gi');\n169\t  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n170\t    if (match[1] !== undefined) names.add(match[1]);\n171\t  }\n172\t  return names;\n173\t}\n174\t\n175\ttype Verdict = 'per-tenant' | 'cross-tenant' | 'enum' | 'retired' | 'unclassified';\n176\t\n177\tfunction classify(\n178\t  identifier: string,\n179\t  registry: Registry,\n180\t  indexes: Map<string, string>,\n181\t  enums: Set<string>,\n182\t): Verdict {\n183\t  if (enums.has(identifier)) return 'enum';\n184\t  if (registry.perTenant.has(identifier)) return 'per-tenant';\n185\t  if (registry.infrastructure.has(identifier)) return 'cross-tenant';\n186\t  if (registry.retired.has(identifier)) return 'retired';\n187\t  const owner = indexes.get(identifier);\n188\t  if (owner !== undefined && owner !== identifier) {\n189\t    return classify(owner, registry, indexes, enums);\n190\t  }\n191\t  return 'unclassified';\n192\t}\n193\t\n194\tinterface Offence {\n195\t  readonly file: string;\n196\t  readonly reason: string;\n197\t}\n198\t\n199\t/** Qualified DDL naming something that must not live in the source schema. */\n200\tfunction qualifiedDdlOffences(file: string, source: string, schema: string): Offence[] {\n201\t  const indexes = indexOwners(source, schema);\n202\t  const enums = enumTypes(source, schema);\n203\t  const registry = registryFor(schema);\n204\t  const offences: Offence[] = [];\n205\t  const seen = new Set<string>();\n206\t\n207\t  const rx = new RegExp(`\"${schema}\"\\\\.\"([^\"]+)\"`, 'g');\n208\t  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n209\t    const identifier = match[1];\n210\t    if (identifier === undefined) continue;\n211\t    // Only DDL counts. A qualified name inside a DML statement, a\n212\t    // `to_regclass()` probe or a RAISE message is not a routing decision.\n213\t    if (!DDL_LEAD.test(source.slice(0, match.index))) continue;\n214\t\n215\t    const verdict = classify(identifier, registry, indexes, enums);\n216\t    if (verdict === 'cross-tenant' || verdict === 'enum' || verdict === 'retired') continue;\n217\t    if (seen.has(identifier)) continue;\n218\t    seen.add(identifier);\n219\t\n220\t    offences.push({\n221\t      file,\n222\t      reason:\n223\t        verdict === 'per-tenant'\n224\t          ? `DDL on \"${schema}\".\"${identifier}\" — a PER-TENANT table (MODULE_SCHEMAS.${schema}.tables). ` +\n225\t            `The replay's search_path cannot redirect a qualified name, so every tenant pass writes to ` +\n226\t            `the source schema and the tenant ledger still records it as applied. Drop the \"${schema}\". prefix.`\n227\t          : `DDL on \"${schema}\".\"${identifier}\" — not in MODULE_SCHEMAS.${schema}.tables OR ` +\n228\t            `.infrastructureTables, so this guard cannot tell whether it belongs in a tenant schema. ` +\n229\t            `Register the table, or add the migration to REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL with a written reason.`,\n230\t    });\n231\t  }\n232\t  return offences;\n233\t}\n234\t\n235\t/**\n236\t * `pinSearchPath(qr, '<own source schema>')` in a migration that is NOT\n237\t * `@SourceOnlyMigration` — the second spelling of the same defect. The helper\n238\t * (`libs/backend-common/src/database/base-migration.ts`) issues\n239\t * `SET search_path TO \"<its literal argument>\", public` and has no tenant\n240\t * awareness, so it OVERRIDES the runner's tenant pin for the rest of the\n241\t * migration. Legitimate on a source-only migration, where the tenant pass skips\n242\t * the file entirely; a silent tenant-data hole anywhere else.\n243\t */\n244\tfunction sourcePinOffences(file: string, source: string, schema: string): Offence[] {\n245\t  if (/@SourceOnlyMigration\\s*\\(/.test(source)) return [];\n246\t  const rx = new RegExp(\n247\t    `\\\\bpinSearchPath\\\\s*\\\\(\\\\s*[A-Za-z_$][\\\\w$]*\\\\s*,\\\\s*['\"\\`]${schema}['\"\\`]`,\n248\t    'g',\n249\t  );\n250\t  if (!rx.test(source)) return [];\n251\t  return [\n252\t    {\n253\t      file,\n254\t      reason:\n255\t        `pinSearchPath(queryRunner, '${schema}') in a migration that is not @SourceOnlyMigration. ` +\n256\t        `It pins its literal argument, so a tenant pass re-pins the SOURCE schema, the DDL no-ops ` +\n257\t        `against rows that already exist there, and the tenant ledger records a successful apply ` +\n258\t        `anyway (MSG-HIGH-077). Either drop the pin and follow the runner's, or mark the migration ` +\n259\t        `@SourceOnlyMigration if the table really is cross-tenant.`,\n260\t    },\n261\t  ];\n262\t}\n263\t\n264\tfunction migrationFiles(): Array<readonly [string, string]> {\n265\t  const args = ['ls-files', ...TENANT_AWARE_MIGRATION_DIRS.map(([dir]) => `${dir}/[0-9]*.ts`)];\n266\t  const files = execFileSync('git', args, { cwd: REPO_ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' })\n267\t    .split('\\n')\n268\t    .map((line) => line.trim())\n269\t    .filter(Boolean);\n270\t\n271\t  return files.map((file) => {\n272\t    const match = TENANT_AWARE_MIGRATION_DIRS.find(([dir]) => file.startsWith(`${dir}/`));\n273\t    if (match === undefined) throw new Error(`no source schema mapped for ${file}`);\n274\t    return [file, match[1]] as const;\n275\t  });\n276\t}\n277\t\n278\tdescribe('tenant-aware migration DDL guard', () => {\n279\t  it('no tenant-aware migration issues source-schema DDL against a per-tenant table', () => {\n280\t    const offences: Offence[] = [];\n281\t    for (const [file, schema] of migrationFiles()) {\n282\t      if (REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL.has(basename(file))) continue;\n283\t      const source = stripComments(readFileSync(join(REPO_ROOT, file), 'utf8'));\n284\t      offences.push(...qualifiedDdlOffences(relative(REPO_ROOT, file), source, schema));\n285\t    }\n286\t\n287\t    expect(offences.map((o) => `${o.file}: ${o.reason}`)).toEqual([]);\n288\t  });\n289\t\n290\t  it('no tenant-aware migration re-pins its own source schema unless it is source-only', () => {\n291\t    const offences: Offence[] = [];\n292\t    for (const [file, schema] of migrationFiles()) {\n293\t      const source = stripComments(readFileSync(join(REPO_ROOT, file), 'utf8'));\n294\t      offences.push(...sourcePinOffences(relative(REPO_ROOT, file), source, schema));\n295\t    }\n296\t\n297\t    expect(offences.map((o) => `${o.file}: ${o.reason}`)).toEqual([]);\n298\t  });\n299\t\n300\t  it('every allowlisted migration still exists (no stale exemptions)', () => {\n301\t    const present = new Set(migrationFiles().map(([f]) => basename(f)));\n302\t    const stale = [...REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL].filter((name) => !present.has(name));\n303\t    expect(stale).toEqual([]);\n304\t  });\n305\t\n306\t  it('every retired-table entry names a table the registry really does not carry', () => {\n307\t    const stale: string[] = [];\n308\t    for (const [schema, tables] of RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES) {\n309\t      const registry = registryFor(schema);\n310\t      for (const table of tables) {\n311\t        if (registry.perTenant.has(table) || registry.infrastructure.has(table)) {\n312\t          stale.push(`${schema}.${table} is registered again — drop it from RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES`);\n313\t        }\n314\t      }\n315\t    }\n316\t    expect(stale).toEqual([]);\n317\t  });\n318\t});\n319\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ff92a1d8-3185-463a-9cf3-02f1306689af","uuid":"10bd1087-65ef-4c0b-ae2c-1be610728908","timestamp":"2026-09-21T02:15:25.226Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-d333052f1946/tests/invariants/tenant-aware-migration-ddl-guard.spec.ts","content":"import { readFileSync } from 'fs';\nimport { basename, join, relative } from 'path';\nimport { execFileSync } from 'child_process';\n\nimport { MODULE_SCHEMAS } from '../../libs/backend-common/src/database/schema-manager.service';\nimport { stripComments } from './helpers/ts-source';\n\n/**\n * Tenant provisioning is migration REPLAY: the orchestrator pins\n * `search_path` to `tenant_<uuid>` and runs each tenant-aware service's\n * migration set. That mechanism only redirects UNQUALIFIED DDL. Anything that\n * names its schema explicitly — or re-pins the session to the source schema —\n * escapes it, lands in the source schema, and is STILL recorded in the tenant's\n * ledger as applied. This spec is the gate for that class.\n *\n * ## Why this spec was rewritten (DATA-HIGH-012)\n *\n * The previous detector was a single regex looking for a DDL keyword followed\n * by a `\"<source schema>\".` identifier, plus a filename allowlist and a blanket\n * exclusion for every Baseline. It enumerated one SPELLING of the defect rather\n * than testing the property, and both halves of that leaked:\n *\n *   - MSG-HIGH-077 shipped a per-tenant migration that re-pinned its own source\n *     schema — functionally identical to qualifying every statement in the file\n *     — CITING THIS GUARD in its docblock as evidence of its own correctness.\n *   - DATA-CRITICAL-010 (no new tenant can be provisioned) lived entirely\n *     inside the Baseline exclusion.\n *\n * It now asks the actual question — *does this migration issue DDL against a\n * PER-TENANT table in the source schema, by either spelling?* — using\n * `MODULE_SCHEMAS` as the classifier. That is the same SSoT the entity layer\n * obeys via `entity-schema-declaration`, so the guard cannot drift from it, and\n * the Baselines are now checked like every other migration rather than excused.\n *\n * `scripts/migration/dequalify-tenant-baselines.mjs` reads the same registry to\n * decide what to rewrite. Two consumers of one SSoT, not two SSoTs.\n *\n * ## Fail-closed\n *\n * A qualified identifier the registry cannot explain is a VIOLATION, not a\n * pass. An unregistered per-tenant table is exactly the shape that would\n * otherwise slip through, so \"I don't recognise this\" resolves against the\n * migration and lands in the reviewer allowlist below with a written reason.\n */\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = join(__dirname, '..', '..');\n\n/** Source schema per tenant-aware migration directory. */\nconst TENANT_AWARE_MIGRATION_DIRS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, string]> = [\n  ['apps/farm-service/src/database/migrations', 'farm'],\n  ['apps/sensor-service/src/database/migrations', 'sensor'],\n  ['apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations', 'hr'],\n  ['apps/messaging-service/src/migrations', 'messaging'],\n  ['apps/messaging-service/src/database/migrations', 'messaging'],\n  ['apps/alert-engine/src/database/migrations', 'alert'],\n  ['apps/ai-service/src/database/migrations', 'ai'],\n  ['apps/hydroponics-service/src/database/migrations', 'hydroponics'],\n];\n\n/** A DDL verb + object immediately preceding a qualified identifier. */\nconst DDL_LEAD =\n  /\\b(?:CREATE|ALTER|DROP|TRUNCATE)\\s+(?:UNIQUE\\s+)?(?:TABLE|INDEX|TYPE|SEQUENCE|VIEW|MATERIALIZED\\s+VIEW)\\b(?:\\s+IF\\s+(?:NOT\\s+)?EXISTS)?[\\s\\S]{0,220}$/i;\n\n/**\n * Tables that exist in a shipped migration's DDL but in NEITHER\n * `MODULE_SCHEMAS[].tables` nor `[].infrastructureTables`, because a later\n * migration retired them. They cannot be classified and they cannot be edited\n * out of an immutable migration, so they are named here with the migration that\n * retired them. A NEW name appearing in a failure means an entity shipped\n * without its registry entry — register it, do not add it here.\n */\nconst RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlySet<string>> = new Map([\n  // Superseded by the `sensor_metrics` hypertable; the per-tenant copy of that\n  // is created unqualified by 1815000000000.\n  ['sensor', new Set(['sensor_readings'])],\n  // Dropped by 1802100000000-DropTenantAiSettings.\n  ['messaging', new Set(['tenant_ai_settings'])],\n]);\n\n/**\n * Migrations whose source-schema-qualified DDL has been REVIEWED as legitimate.\n * This is a reviewer-gated allowlist in THIS spec, not a self-service docblock\n * marker: ORPHAN-HIGH-408's `CreateAiProposedActions` used the old\n * `TENANT_AWARE_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL_OK` docblock marker to schema-qualify a\n * PER-TENANT table, which the replay then never landed in any tenant. A\n * self-service marker bypasses CI silently; an allowlist edit shows up in the\n * PR diff. Adding an entry must be justified in writing.\n */\nconst REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([\n  // Source-only enum reclaim/heal of the 2026-07-07 QualityGrade outage.\n  '1804400000000-DropOrphanQualityGradeEnum.ts',\n  '1804500000000-HealBehindTenantQualityGrade.ts',\n  // Cross-tenant source-schema tables + partition-contract index (not cloned).\n  '1800300000000-SensorV2TenantFkAndLicenseGrant.ts',\n  '1802000000000-AddVfdDeviceModelSeriesPumpTags.ts',\n  '1804000000000-ConsolidateVfdRegisterMappingsToSensorSchema.ts',\n  // SENSOR-CRITICAL-007 Slice 1: nullable edge-binding columns on the per-tenant\n  // vfd_devices table via the SAME source-template + tenant fan-out pattern as\n  // 1802000000000 above (canonical `sensor` copy + every `tenant_*` copy). Not a\n  // per-tenant table wrongly schema-qualified — the fan-out lands it everywhere.\n  '1808000000000-AddVfdDeviceEdgeBinding.ts',\n  // SEC-MEDIUM-083: partial unique index on the per-tenant vfd_change_sets\n  // table — canonical `sensor` copy + tenant fan-out (same pattern as\n  // 1808000000000 above). Per-tenant, not misqualified.\n  '1818000000000-EnforceSingleActiveVfdChangeSet.ts',\n  // SENSOR-HIGH-064: nullable config-ack tracking columns on the per-tenant\n  // edge_devices table via the SAME source-template + tenant fan-out pattern\n  // (canonical `sensor` copy + every `tenant_*` copy).\n  '1809000000000-AddEdgeDeviceConfigAckTracking.ts',\n  // SENSOR-HIGH-083: per-tenant calibration_events table + nullable\n  // calibration_interval_days column on sensor_data_channels, both created in the\n  // canonical `sensor` source schema and fanned out into every `tenant_*` schema\n  // (CREATE TABLE … LIKE INCLUDING ALL for the clone). Per-tenant, not misqualified.\n  '1810000000000-AddCalibrationEventsAndInterval.ts',\n  '1803000000000-HashProvisioningSecretsAtRest.ts',\n  '1800500000000-EnsureMessagingPartitionContract.ts',\n  // ORPHAN-HIGH-408: immutable historical BUG — `ai_proposed_actions` is a\n  // per-tenant table wrongly schema-qualified. Retained only because migrations\n  // are immutable; HEALED by 1803100000000-HealAiProposedActionsUnqualified.\n  // DO NOT copy this pattern: per-tenant tables must be UNQUALIFIED.\n  '1803000000000-CreateAiProposedActions.ts',\n]);\n\ninterface Registry {\n  readonly perTenant: ReadonlySet<string>;\n  readonly infrastructure: ReadonlySet<string>;\n  readonly retired: ReadonlySet<string>;\n}\n\nfunction registryFor(schema: string): Registry {\n  const entry = MODULE_SCHEMAS.find((m) => m.sourceSchema === schema);\n  if (entry === undefined) {\n    throw new Error(`MODULE_SCHEMAS has no entry for source schema \"${schema}\"`);\n  }\n  return {\n    perTenant: new Set([...entry.tables, ...(entry.referenceDataTables ?? [])]),\n    infrastructure: new Set(entry.infrastructureTables ?? []),\n    retired: RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES.get(schema) ?? new Set<string>(),\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * `CREATE [UNIQUE] INDEX \"name\" ON \"schema\".\"table\"` — an index inherits its\n * table's classification, and `DROP INDEX \"schema\".\"name\"` names no table, so\n * the map has to be built from the create side first.\n */\nfunction indexOwners(source: string, schema: string): Map<string, string> {\n  const owners = new Map<string, string>();\n  const rx = new RegExp(\n    `CREATE\\\\s+(?:UNIQUE\\\\s+)?INDEX\\\\s+(?:IF\\\\s+NOT\\\\s+EXISTS\\\\s+)?\"([^\"]+)\"\\\\s+ON\\\\s+(?:\"${schema}\"\\\\.)?\"([^\"]+)\"`,\n    'gi',\n  );\n  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n    if (match[1] !== undefined && match[2] !== undefined) owners.set(match[1], match[2]);\n  }\n  return owners;\n}\n\n/**\n * Enum type names from their own definitions rather than from a naming\n * convention — farm's `equipment_category` is an enum and carries no `_enum`\n * suffix. Enums are DELIBERATELY left qualified: existing tenants SHARE the\n * source-schema type (`CREATE TABLE … LIKE INCLUDING ALL` shares a type, it does\n * not clone it), so de-qualifying would split the fleet in two.\n */\nfunction enumTypes(source: string, schema: string): Set<string> {\n  const names = new Set<string>();\n  const rx = new RegExp(`CREATE\\\\s+TYPE\\\\s+\"${schema}\"\\\\.\"([^\"]+)\"\\\\s+AS\\\\s+ENUM`, 'gi');\n  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n    if (match[1] !== undefined) names.add(match[1]);\n  }\n  return names;\n}\n\ntype Verdict = 'per-tenant' | 'cross-tenant' | 'enum' | 'retired' | 'unclassified';\n\nfunction classify(\n  identifier: string,\n  registry: Registry,\n  indexes: Map<string, string>,\n  enums: Set<string>,\n): Verdict {\n  if (enums.has(identifier)) return 'enum';\n  if (registry.perTenant.has(identifier)) return 'per-tenant';\n  if (registry.infrastructure.has(identifier)) return 'cross-tenant';\n  if (registry.retired.has(identifier)) return 'retired';\n  const owner = indexes.get(identifier);\n  if (owner !== undefined && owner !== identifier) {\n    return classify(owner, registry, indexes, enums);\n  }\n  return 'unclassified';\n}\n\ninterface Offence {\n  readonly file: string;\n  readonly reason: string;\n}\n\n/** Qualified DDL naming something that must not live in the source schema. */\nfunction qualifiedDdlOffences(file: string, source: string, schema: string): Offence[] {\n  const indexes = indexOwners(source, schema);\n  const enums = enumTypes(source, schema);\n  const registry = registryFor(schema);\n  const offences: Offence[] = [];\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n\n  const rx = new RegExp(`\"${schema}\"\\\\.\"([^\"]+)\"`, 'g');\n  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n    const identifier = match[1];\n    if (identifier === undefined) continue;\n    // Only DDL counts. A qualified name inside a DML statement, a\n    // `to_regclass()` probe or a RAISE message is not a routing decision.\n    if (!DDL_LEAD.test(source.slice(0, match.index))) continue;\n\n    const verdict = classify(identifier, registry, indexes, enums);\n    if (verdict === 'cross-tenant' || verdict === 'enum' || verdict === 'retired') continue;\n    if (seen.has(identifier)) continue;\n    seen.add(identifier);\n\n    offences.push({\n      file,\n      reason:\n        verdict === 'per-tenant'\n          ? `DDL on \"${schema}\".\"${identifier}\" — a PER-TENANT table (MODULE_SCHEMAS.${schema}.tables). ` +\n            `The replay's search_path cannot redirect a qualified name, so every tenant pass writes to ` +\n            `the source schema and the tenant ledger still records it as applied. Drop the \"${schema}\". prefix.`\n          : `DDL on \"${schema}\".\"${identifier}\" — not in MODULE_SCHEMAS.${schema}.tables OR ` +\n            `.infrastructureTables, so this guard cannot tell whether it belongs in a tenant schema. ` +\n            `Register the table, or add the migration to REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL with a written reason.`,\n    });\n  }\n  return offences;\n}\n\n/**\n * `pinSearchPath(qr, '<own source schema>')` in a migration that is NOT\n * `@SourceOnlyMigration` — the second spelling of the same defect. The helper\n * (`libs/backend-common/src/database/base-migration.ts`) issues\n * `SET search_path TO \"<its literal argument>\", public` and has no tenant\n * awareness, so it OVERRIDES the runner's tenant pin for the rest of the\n * migration. Legitimate on a source-only migration, where the tenant pass skips\n * the file entirely; a silent tenant-data hole anywhere else.\n */\nfunction sourcePinOffences(file: string, source: string, schema: string): Offence[] {\n  if (/@SourceOnlyMigration\\s*\\(/.test(source)) return [];\n  const rx = new RegExp(\n    `\\\\bpinSearchPath\\\\s*\\\\(\\\\s*[A-Za-z_$][\\\\w$]*\\\\s*,\\\\s*['\"\\`]${schema}['\"\\`]`,\n    'g',\n  );\n  if (!rx.test(source)) return [];\n  return [\n    {\n      file,\n      reason:\n        `pinSearchPath(queryRunner, '${schema}') in a migration that is not @SourceOnlyMigration. ` +\n        `It pins its literal argument, so a tenant pass re-pins the SOURCE schema, the DDL no-ops ` +\n        `against rows that already exist there, and the tenant ledger records a successful apply ` +\n        `anyway (MSG-HIGH-077). Either drop the pin and follow the runner's, or mark the migration ` +\n        `@SourceOnlyMigration if the table really is cross-tenant.`,\n    },\n  ];\n}\n\nfunction migrationFiles(): Array<readonly [string, string]> {\n  const args = ['ls-files', ...TENANT_AWARE_MIGRATION_DIRS.map(([dir]) => `${dir}/[0-9]*.ts`)];\n  const files = execFileSync('git', args, { cwd: REPO_ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' })\n    .split('\\n')\n    .map((line) => line.trim())\n    .filter(Boolean);\n\n  return files.map((file) => {\n    const match = TENANT_AWARE_MIGRATION_DIRS.find(([dir]) => file.startsWith(`${dir}/`));\n    if (match === undefined) throw new Error(`no source schema mapped for ${file}`);\n    return [file, match[1]] as const;\n  });\n}\n\ndescribe('tenant-aware migration DDL guard', () => {\n  it('no tenant-aware migration issues source-schema DDL against a per-tenant table', () => {\n    const offences: Offence[] = [];\n    for (const [file, schema] of migrationFiles()) {\n      if (REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL.has(basename(file))) continue;\n      const source = stripComments(readFileSync(join(REPO_ROOT, file), 'utf8'));\n      offences.push(...qualifiedDdlOffences(relative(REPO_ROOT, file), source, schema));\n    }\n\n    expect(offences.map((o) => `${o.file}: ${o.reason}`)).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('no tenant-aware migration re-pins its own source schema unless it is source-only', () => {\n    const offences: Offence[] = [];\n    for (const [file, schema] of migrationFiles()) {\n      const source = stripComments(readFileSync(join(REPO_ROOT, file), 'utf8'));\n      offences.push(...sourcePinOffences(relative(REPO_ROOT, file), source, schema));\n    }\n\n    expect(offences.map((o) => `${o.file}: ${o.reason}`)).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('every allowlisted migration still exists (no stale exemptions)', () => {\n    const present = new Set(migrationFiles().map(([f]) => basename(f)));\n    const stale = [...REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL].filter((name) => !present.has(name));\n    expect(stale).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('every retired-table entry names a table the registry really does not carry', () => {\n    const stale: string[] = [];\n    for (const [schema, tables] of RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES) {\n      const registry = registryFor(schema);\n      for (const table of tables) {\n        if (registry.perTenant.has(table) || registry.infrastructure.has(table)) {\n          stale.push(`${schema}.${table} is registered again — drop it from RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES`);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    expect(stale).toEqual([]);\n  });\n});\n","numLines":319,"startLine":1,"totalLines":319}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CaVE5FWwGxT8B35gmzrizw","type":"tool_result","content":"200\t *     timestamped TypeORM migration files. Two patterns exist in this\n201\t *     codebase (historical drift, ADR-011 §\"Migration Runners\"):\n202\t *       * `apps/<svc>/src/migrations/`         (auth, admin-api, messaging, event-store)\n203\t *       * `apps/<svc>/src/database/migrations/` (everything else)\n204\t *     This manifest reflects the actual on-disk layout — keep in sync\n205\t *     when adding a service.\n206\t *\n207\t * Order matters: services with cross-schema FK migrations (e.g.\n208\t * messaging.tenant_ai_settings -> auth.tenants) must run after the\n209\t * schema they reference. We run auth FIRST for that reason; everything\n210\t * else is alphabetical.\n211\t */\n212\tinterface ServiceManifest {\n213\t  name: string;\n214\t  schema: string;\n215\t  migrationsDir: string;\n216\t  /**\n217\t   * `entitiesGlob`: optional glob (relative to repo root) that matches\n218\t   * every `*.entity.ts` file owned by this service. The April drift incident was caused by entity shape\n6\t * advancing past the DB contract while runtime synchronize and ghost SQL hid the\n7\t * gap. This invariant locks the repaired contract in source control:\n8\t *\n9\t * - no service-local `init-*schema*.sql` DDL authority can exist;\n10\t * - the active messaging migrations carry the columns called out by\n11\t *   INFRA-CRITICAL-011.\n12\t */\n13\timport { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\n14\timport { join, relative, resolve } from 'node:path';\n15\t\n16\tconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\n17\tconst APPS_DIR = resolve(REPO_ROOT, 'apps');\n18\tconst MESSAGING_MIGRATIONS_DIR = resolve(\n19\t  REPO_ROOT,\n20\t  'apps',\n21\t  'messaging-service',\n22\t  'src',\n23\t  'migrations',\n24\t);\n25\t\n26\tfunction listFiles(dir: string): string[] {\n27\t  const out: string[] = [];\n28\t  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {\n29\t    const full = join(dir, entry);\n30\t    const stat = statSync(full);\n31\t    if (stat.isDirectory()) {\n32\t      if (entry === 'node_modules' || entry === '.archive') continue;\n33\t      out.push(...listFiles(full));\n34\t      continue;\n35\t    }\n36\t    if (stat.isFile()) out.push(full);\n37\t  }\n38\t  return out;\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\tfunction activeMessagingMigrationCorpus(): string {\n42\t  return readdirSync(MESSAGING_MIGRATIONS_DIR)\n43\t    .filter((entry) => entry.endsWith('.ts'))\n44\t    .sort()\n45\t    .map((entry) => readFileSync(join(MESSAGING_MIGRATIONS_DIR, entry), 'utf8'))\n46\t    .join('\\n');\n47\t}\n48\t\n49\tfunction expectMigrationSql(label: string, pattern: RegExp): void {\n50\t  const corpus = activeMessagingMigrationCorpus();\n51\t  if (!pattern.test(corpus)) {\n52\t    throw new Error(\n53\t      `Messaging migration SSoT is missing ${label}.\\n` +\n54\t        `Fix the TypeORM migration ledger; do not add service-local init SQL.`,\n55\t    );\n56\t  }\n57\t}\n58\t\n59\tdescribe('messaging schema SSoT (INFRA-CRITICAL-011)', () => {\n60\t  it('does not carry service-local init schema SQL as a parallel DDL source', () => {\n61\t    const violations = listFiles(APPS_DIR)\n62\t      .map((file) => relative(REPO_ROOT, file))\n63\t      .filter((file) => /(^|\\/)init-[^/]*schema[^/]*\\.sql$/i.test(file));\n64\t\n65\t    expect(violations).toEqual([]);\n66\t  });\n67\t\n68\t  /**\n69\t   * A per-tenant table's DDL must be UNQUALIFIED so tenant provisioning — which\n70\t   * is migration replay with `search_path` pinned to `tenant_<uuid>` — routes it\n71\t   * into the tenant schema instead of writing back into `messaging`\n72\t   * (DATA-CRITICAL-010). Pinning the qualified spelling here is what let the\n73\t   * regression look correct, so the shape is now part of the assertion rather\n74\t   * than incidental to it.\n75\t   */\n76\t  const perTenantColumn = (table: string, column: RegExp): RegExp =>\n77\t    new RegExp(`CREATE TABLE (?:IF NOT EXISTS )?\"${table}\" \\\\([^\\`]*${column.source}`, 'i');\n78\t\n79\t  const expectedMigrationPatterns: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, RegExp]> = [\n80\t    ['channel_members.tenantId NOT NULL', perTenantColumn('channel_members', /\"tenantId\" uuid NOT NULL/)],\n81\t    ['channels.tenantId NOT NULL', perTenantColumn('channels', /\"tenantId\" uuid NOT NULL/)],\n82\t    ['messages.tenantId NOT NULL', perTenantColumn('messages', /\"tenantId\" uuid NOT NULL/)],\n83\t /\"legalMatterDescription\" text/),\n102\t    ],\n103\t    ['legal_holds.requestedBy', perTenantColumn('legal_holds', /\"requestedBy\" uuid/)],\n104\t    [\n105\t      'legal_holds.expiresAt',\n106\t * Messaging schema SSoT invariant - INFRA-CRITICAL-011.\n *\n * The messaging database shape is owned by the TypeORM migration ledger, not by\n * service-local init SQL. The April drift incident was caused by entity shape\n * advancing past the DB contract while runtime synchronize and ghost SQL hid the\n * gap. This invariant locks the repaired contract in source control:\n *\n * - no service-local `init-*schema*.sql` DDL authority can exist;\n * - the active messaging migrations carry the columns called out by\n *   INFRA-CRITICAL-011.\n */\nimport { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { join, relative, resolve } from 'node:path';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\nconst APPS_DIR = resolve(REPO_ROOT, 'apps');\nconst MESSAGING_MIGRATIONS_DIR = resolve(\n  REPO_ROOT,\n  'apps',\n  'messaging-service',\n  'src',\n  'migrations',\n);\n\nfunction listFiles(dir: string): string[] {\n  const out: string[] = [];\n  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {\n    const full = join(dir, entry);\n    const stat = statSync(full);\n    if (stat.isDirectory()) {\n      if (entry === 'node_modules' || entry === '.archive') continue;\n      out.push(...listFiles(full));\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (stat.isFile()) out.push(full);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nfunction activeMessagingMigrationCorpus(): string {\n  return readdirSync(MESSAGING_MIGRATIONS_DIR)\n    .filter((entry) => entry.endsWith('.ts'))\n    .sort()\n    .map((entry) => readFileSync(join(MESSAGING_MIGRATIONS_DIR, entry), 'utf8'))\n    .join('\\n');\n}\n\nfunction expectMigrationSql(label: string, pattern: RegExp): void {\n  const corpus = activeMessagingMigrationCorpus();\n  if (!pattern.test(corpus)) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `Messaging migration SSoT is missing ${label}.\\n` +\n        `Fix the TypeORM migration ledger; do not add service-local init SQL.`,\n    );\n  }\n}\n\ndescribe('messaging schema SSoT (INFRA-CRITICAL-011)', () => {\n  it('does not carry service-local init schema SQL as a parallel DDL source', () => {\n    const violations = listFiles(APPS_DIR)\n      .map((file) => relative(REPO_ROOT, file))\n      .filter((file) => /(^|\\/)init-[^/]*schema[^/]*\\.sql$/i.test(file));\n\n    expect(violations).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  /**\n   * A per-tenant table's DDL must be UNQUALIFIED so tenant provisioning — which\n   * is migration replay with `search_path` pinned to `tenant_<uuid>` — routes it\n   * into the tenant schema instead of writing back into `messaging`\n   * (DATA-CRITICAL-010). Pinning the qualified spelling here is what let the\n   * regression look correct, so the shape is now part of the assertion rather\n   * than incidental to it.\n   */\n  const perTenantColumn = (table: string, column: RegExp): RegExp =>\n    new RegExp(`CREATE TABLE (?:IF NOT EXISTS )?\"${table}\" \\\\([^\\`]*${column.source}`, 'i');\n\n  const expectedMigrationPatterns: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, RegExp]> = [\n    ['channel_members.tenantId NOT NULL', perTenantColumn('channel_members', /\"tenantId\" uuid NOT NULL/)],\n    ['channels.tenantId NOT NULL', perTenantColumn('channels', /\"tenantId\" uuid NOT NULL/)],\n    ['messages.tenantId NOT NULL', perTenantColumn('messages', /\"tenantId\" uuid NOT NULL/)],\n    [\n      'messages.isAiGenerated NOT NULL default',\n      perTenantColumn('messages', /\"isAiGenerated\" boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false/),\n    ],\n    [\n      'message_attachments.is_deleted NOT NULL default',\n      perTenantColumn('message_attachments', /\"is_deleted\" boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false/),\n    ],\n    [\n      'message_attachments.deleted_at',\n      perTenantColumn('message_attachments', /\"deleted_at\" TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE/),\n    ],\n    [\n      'legal_holds.legalMatterId NOT NULL',\n      perTenantColumn('legal_holds', /\"legalMatterId\" uuid NOT NULL/),\n    ],\n    [\n      'legal_holds.legalMatterDescription',\n      perTenantColumn('legal_holds', /\"legalMatterDescription\" text/),\n    ],\n    ['legal_holds.requestedBy', perTenantColumn('legal_holds', /\"requestedBy\" uuid/)],\n    [\n      'legal_holds.expiresAt',\n      perTenantColumn('legal_holds', /\"expiresAt\" TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE/),\n    ],\n    // Cross-tenant infrastructure: the outbox stays in `messaging` by design,\n    // so its DDL is the one that KEEPS the schema qualifier.\n    [\n      'messaging_outbox.isDeadLettered NOT NULL default',\n      /messaging\\.messaging_outbox[^`]*\"isDeadLettered\" BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false/i,\n    ],\n  ];\n\n  it.each(expectedMigrationPatterns)('%s lives in the active migration ledger', (label, pattern) {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ALBJHzGSqawhDXWbqr9uwH","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Bootstrap From Scratch\n3\t * ============================================================================\n4\t *\n5\t * Proves that the fresh-volume init pipeline — `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/*`\n6\t * + every service's TypeORM migration chain — produces a clean, complete\n7\t * platform schema when run against an empty Postgres.\n8\t *\n9\t * # Why this test exists\n10\t *\n11\t * The codebase's bootstrap pipeline silently broke when several\n12\t * `CREATE TABLE` migrations were squashed out of source. Production survived\n13\t * because nobody did a fresh-volume init for months — every existing\n14\t * environment had the squashed-out tables already on disk. The first\n15\t * fresh-volume deploy crashed at `1781100000000-ConvertTimestampToTimestamptz`\n16\t * trying to ALTER `auth.users.mfaLockedUntil` (a column whose CREATE was\n17\t * gone). That is the exact regression class this invariant guards against.\n18\t *\n19\t * # What it does\n20\t *\n21\t *   1. Spin up `timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16` (the production image, by\n22\t *      digest pinned to docker-compose.infra.yml).\n23\t *   2. Copy `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/` into\n24\t *      `/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/` — Postgres runs every script in\n25\t *      lexical order on first boot of an empty PGDATA volume.\n26\t *   3. Connect, then for every service that owns a TypeORM migration\n27\t *      directory, dynamically load every migration class and run them\n28\t *      through a per-service DataSource bound to that service's schema.\n29\t *   4. Assert schema-level invariants: schemas exist, the previously\n30\t *      missing baseline tables now exist, the previously missing\n31\t *      `auth.users` columns now exist, the sensor hypertable was\n32\t *      created, db-migrate can owner-align a real tenant's continuous\n33\t *      aggregates, and each service's migration ledger is non-empty\n34\t *      (proving the migrations actually ran).\n35\t *\n36\t * # When this test fails\n37\t *\n38\t *   - A migration that creates a baseline table was squashed →\n39\t *     restore the CREATE in the earliest service migration. (This is\n40\t *     the original regression class.)\n41\t *   - An init-script SQL file was deleted without an equivalent\n42\t *     migration → either restore the script OR write a migration that\n43\t *     creates the same tables idempotently.\n44\t *   - A new entity was added without a corresponding CREATE TABLE\n45\t *     migration AND without an init-script entry → add the migration.\n46\t *   - The init scripts assume a privilege the bootstrap POSTGRES_USER\n47\t *     does not have on a fresh container → fix the script to use\n48\t *     a less-privileged path or supply additional bootstrap material.\n49\t *\n50\t * # Why testcontainers (not the shared TestDatabase pool)\n51\t *\n52\t * The other e2e/integration specs assert state of an *already-running*\n53\t * database that the dev/CI infra brought up — they're checking\n54\t * application-level behaviour. This test asserts the bootstrap ITSELF\n55\t * works on an EMPTY volume. It MUST start with no PGDATA, run the init\n56\t * scripts, then run the migration chain. testcontainers is the only\n57\t * way to get that hermetic guarantee inside a Jest worker.\n58\t *\n59\t * # Runtime\n60\t *\n61\t * ~3-5 minutes on a cold runner: image pull + init-script execution +\n62\t * 100+ migrations across 13 services. CI gate runs only on PRs that\n63\t * touch `apps/*\\/src/**\\/migrations/**` or\n64\t * `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**` (see CI workflow\n65\t * `.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml` integration; if the gate\n66\t * is not yet wired, `npm run test:bootstrap` is the local entry point).\n67\t */\n68\t\n69\timport { readdirSync, statSync, existsSync } from 'fs';\n70\timport { createRequire } from 'module';\n71\timport { join, resolve } from 'path';\n72\t\n73\timport { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\n74\timport { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\n75\timport { GenericContainer, type StartedTestContainer, Wait } from 'testcontainers';\n76\timport { DataSource, type MigrationInterface } from 'typeorm';\n77\t\n78\t// ADR-031 Platform Bootstrap Atom — runs the schema/role/extension/function/\n79\t// shared-table DDL contract that init-scripts USED to own pre-cutover. The\n80\t// init-scripts directory copied below now contains only `01-init-databases.sql`\n81\t// (initdb-only DB GRANTs). Schemas, roles, functions, and shared.* tables\n82\t// are created by the call to runPlatformBootstrap() below before any\n83\t// per-service migration loop runs.\n84\timport {\n85\t  runPlatformBootstrap,\n86\t  resolvePlatformBootstrapSqlDir,\n87\t} from '../platform-bootstrap.service';\n88\timport { ensureTenantSensorContinuousAggregateAuthority } from '../tenant-sensor-continuous-aggregate-authority';\n89\t\n90\t// Constructor type for the @Entity / migration classes this spec loads\n91\t// dynamically. TypeORM's DataSource `entities`/`migrations` options accept\n92\t// `Function`, but `@typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-function-type` (correctly)\n93\t// rejects the bare `Function` type; a precise construct signature is both\n94\t// assignable to TypeORM's `Function` slot AND keeps the collected values\n95\t// typed as classes rather than arbitrary callables.\n96\ttype EntityClass = new (...args: never[]) => object;\n97\t\n98\t// Synchronous module loader bound to this spec's location. `import`/`await\n99\t// import()` cannot be used here: loadMigrationClassesFromDir is invoked inside\n100\t// `it.each(...)` arguments that Jest evaluates synchronously at collection\n101\t// time, so the load must be synchronous. `createRequire(__filename)` resolves\n102\t// migration classes and the cross-package schema-drift-validator through\n103\t// ts-jest at runtime, so the static type-check graph never reaches across\n104\t// project rootDirs — the same dynamic-load pattern the db-migrate\n105\t// platform-bootstrap.integration.spec.ts relies on.\n106\tconst requireModule = createRequire(__filename);\n107\t\n108\t// Structured logger for the (rare) entity-load skip path — `console.*` is\n109\t// banned platform-wide; the NestJS Logger keeps the diagnostic on the same\n110\t// structured-logging path the runtime services use.\n111\tconst logger = new Logger('bootstrap-from-scratch');\n112\t\n113\t// Local narrowing helper. db-migrate has @typescript-eslint/no-non-null-assertion\n114\t// ENABLED (unlike the e2e project, whose assertDefined SSoT this replaces), so a\n115\t// bare `!` is banned here. assertDefined narrows `T | undefined` to `T` and fails\n116\t// loudly at the assertion site instead of surfacing as a cryptic\n117\t// \"cannot read properties of undefined\" further downstream.\n118\tfunction assertDefined<T>(value: T | null | undefined, message?: string): T {\n119\t  if (value === null || value === undefined) {\n120\t    throw new Error(message ?? 'assertDefined: expected a value but received null/undefined');\n121\t  }\n122\t  return value;\n123\t}\n124\t\n125\t// Dynamic load of the backend-common schema-drift-validator. Importing\n126\t// schema-drift-validator.service.ts directly via `import { ... }` would pull a\n127\t// file from another Nx project into db-migrate's type-check graph. ts-jest\n128\t// resolves this lazily at runtime (same pattern the migration loader uses for\n129\t// migration classes), so the validator's stable runtime contract is exercised\n130\t// without a static cross-project import. From this spec's location\n131\t// (apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/) the repo root is four levels up.\n132\ttype SchemaDriftValidatorFactory = (\n133\t  serviceName: string,\n134\t) => new (ds: DataSource, cs: ConfigService) => { onApplicationBootstrap(): Promise<void> };\n135\tfunction loadDriftValidatorFactory(): SchemaDriftValidatorFactory {\n136\t  const mod = requireModule(\n137\t    '../../../../libs/backend-common/src/database/schema-drift-validator.service',\n138\t  ) as {\n139\t    createSchemaDriftValidator: SchemaDriftValidatorFactory;\n140\t  };\n141\t  return mod.createSchemaDriftValidator;\n142\t}\n143\t\n144\t// The same image+digest the production stack uses — see\n145\t// docker-compose.infra.yml line 12. Pinning by digest guarantees the\n146\t// init-script behaviour we're proving here matches what runs in\n147\t// production. Bumping the digest is a deliberate review surface.\n148\tconst POSTGRES_IMAGE =\n149\t  'timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16@sha256:b3d038d0a0757df8a5ec0a94ba68d9ad57b0e16100a024cf4b370c77ad5645f7';\n150\t\n151\tconst DATABASE_NAME = 'aquaculture';\n152\tconst DATABASE_USER = 'aquaculture';\n153\tconst DATABASE_PASSWORD = 'aquaculture-test';\n154\tconst DB_MIGRATE_DDL_AUTHORITY_ENV = 'DB_MIGRATE_DDL_AUTHORITY';\n155\tconst SERVICE_ROLE_PASSWORD_ENVS = [\n156\t  'AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n157\t  'FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n158\t  'SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n159\t  'BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n160\t  'HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n161\t  'ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n162\t  'ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n163\t  'GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n164\t  'NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n165\t  'HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n166\t  'AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n167\t  'MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n168\t  'OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n169\t  'EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n170\t  'CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n171\t] as const;\n172\t\n173\t// Repo root, derived from this file's location:\n174\t//   apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts -> ../../../..\n175\t// (mirrors how the sibling platform-bootstrap.integration.spec.ts resolves Two patterns exist in this"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ff92a1d8-3185-463a-9cf3-02f1306689af","uuid":"47228341-83e6-4555-990e-e6e1df372aee","timestamp":"2026-09-21T02:16:08.573Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-d333052f1946/apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * Bootstrap From Scratch\n * ============================================================================\n *\n * Proves that the fresh-volume init pipeline — `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/*`\n * + every service's TypeORM migration chain — produces a clean, complete\n * platform schema when run against an empty Postgres.\n *\n * # Why this test exists\n *\n * The codebase's bootstrap pipeline silently broke when several\n * `CREATE TABLE` migrations were squashed out of source. Production survived\n * because nobody did a fresh-volume init for months — every existing\n * environment had the squashed-out tables already on disk. The first\n * fresh-volume deploy crashed at `1781100000000-ConvertTimestampToTimestamptz`\n * trying to ALTER `auth.users.mfaLockedUntil` (a column whose CREATE was\n * gone). That is the exact regression class this invariant guards against.\n *\n * # What it does\n *\n *   1. Spin up `timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16` (the production image, by\n *      digest pinned to docker-compose.infra.yml).\n *   2. Copy `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/` into\n *      `/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/` — Postgres runs every script in\n *      lexical order on first boot of an empty PGDATA volume.\n *   3. Connect, then for every service that owns a TypeORM migration\n *      directory, dynamically load every migration class and run them\n *      through a per-service DataSource bound to that service's schema.\n *   4. Assert schema-level invariants: schemas exist, the previously\n *      missing baseline tables now exist, the previously missing\n *      `auth.users` columns now exist, the sensor hypertable was\n *      created, db-migrate can owner-align a real tenant's continuous\n *      aggregates, and each service's migration ledger is non-empty\n *      (proving the migrations actually ran).\n *\n * # When this test fails\n *\n *   - A migration that creates a baseline table was squashed →\n *     restore the CREATE in the earliest service migration. (This is\n *     the original regression class.)\n *   - An init-script SQL file was deleted without an equivalent\n *     migration → either restore the script OR write a migration that\n *     creates the same tables idempotently.\n *   - A new entity was added without a corresponding CREATE TABLE\n *     migration AND without an init-script entry → add the migration.\n *   - The init scripts assume a privilege the bootstrap POSTGRES_USER\n *     does not have on a fresh container → fix the script to use\n *     a less-privileged path or supply additional bootstrap material.\n *\n * # Why testcontainers (not the shared TestDatabase pool)\n *\n * The other e2e/integration specs assert state of an *already-running*\n * database that the dev/CI infra brought up — they're checking\n * application-level behaviour. This test asserts the bootstrap ITSELF\n * works on an EMPTY volume. It MUST start with no PGDATA, run the init\n * scripts, then run the migration chain. testcontainers is the only\n * way to get that hermetic guarantee inside a Jest worker.\n *\n * # Runtime\n *\n * ~3-5 minutes on a cold runner: image pull + init-script execution +\n * 100+ migrations across 13 services. CI gate runs only on PRs that\n * touch `apps/*\\/src/**\\/migrations/**` or\n * `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**` (see CI workflow\n * `.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml` integration; if the gate\n * is not yet wired, `npm run test:bootstrap` is the local entry point).\n */\n\nimport { readdirSync, statSync, existsSync } from 'fs';\nimport { createRequire } from 'module';\nimport { join, resolve } from 'path';\n\nimport { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\nimport { GenericContainer, type StartedTestContainer, Wait } from 'testcontainers';\nimport { DataSource, type MigrationInterface } from 'typeorm';\n\n// ADR-031 Platform Bootstrap Atom — runs the schema/role/extension/function/\n// shared-table DDL contract that init-scripts USED to own pre-cutover. The\n// init-scripts directory copied below now contains only `01-init-databases.sql`\n// (initdb-only DB GRANTs). Schemas, roles, functions, and shared.* tables\n// are created by the call to runPlatformBootstrap() below before any\n// per-service migration loop runs.\nimport {\n  runPlatformBootstrap,\n  resolvePlatformBootstrapSqlDir,\n} from '../platform-bootstrap.service';\nimport { ensureTenantSensorContinuousAggregateAuthority } from '../tenant-sensor-continuous-aggregate-authority';\n\n// Constructor type for the @Entity / migration classes this spec loads\n// dynamically. TypeORM's DataSource `entities`/`migrations` options accept\n// `Function`, but `@typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-function-type` (correctly)\n// rejects the bare `Function` type; a precise construct signature is both\n// assignable to TypeORM's `Function` slot AND keeps the collected values\n// typed as classes rather than arbitrary callables.\ntype EntityClass = new (...args: never[]) => object;\n\n// Synchronous module loader bound to this spec's location. `import`/`await\n// import()` cannot be used here: loadMigrationClassesFromDir is invoked inside\n// `it.each(...)` arguments that Jest evaluates synchronously at collection\n// time, so the load must be synchronous. `createRequire(__filename)` resolves\n// migration classes and the cross-package schema-drift-validator through\n// ts-jest at runtime, so the static type-check graph never reaches across\n// project rootDirs — the same dynamic-load pattern the db-migrate\n// platform-bootstrap.integration.spec.ts relies on.\nconst requireModule = createRequire(__filename);\n\n// Structured logger for the (rare) entity-load skip path — `console.*` is\n// banned platform-wide; the NestJS Logger keeps the diagnostic on the same\n// structured-logging path the runtime services use.\nconst logger = new Logger('bootstrap-from-scratch');\n\n// Local narrowing helper. db-migrate has @typescript-eslint/no-non-null-assertion\n// ENABLED (unlike the e2e project, whose assertDefined SSoT this replaces), so a\n// bare `!` is banned here. assertDefined narrows `T | undefined` to `T` and fails\n// loudly at the assertion site instead of surfacing as a cryptic\n// \"cannot read properties of undefined\" further downstream.\nfunction assertDefined<T>(value: T | null | undefined, message?: string): T {\n  if (value === null || value === undefined) {\n    throw new Error(message ?? 'assertDefined: expected a value but received null/undefined');\n  }\n  return value;\n}\n\n// Dynamic load of the backend-common schema-drift-validator. Importing\n// schema-drift-validator.service.ts directly via `import { ... }` would pull a\n// file from another Nx project into db-migrate's type-check graph. ts-jest\n// resolves this lazily at runtime (same pattern the migration loader uses for\n// migration classes), so the validator's stable runtime contract is exercised\n// without a static cross-project import. From this spec's location\n// (apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/) the repo root is four levels up.\ntype SchemaDriftValidatorFactory = (\n  serviceName: string,\n) => new (ds: DataSource, cs: ConfigService) => { onApplicationBootstrap(): Promise<void> };\nfunction loadDriftValidatorFactory(): SchemaDriftValidatorFactory {\n  const mod = requireModule(\n    '../../../../libs/backend-common/src/database/schema-drift-validator.service',\n  ) as {\n    createSchemaDriftValidator: SchemaDriftValidatorFactory;\n  };\n  return mod.createSchemaDriftValidator;\n}\n\n// The same image+digest the production stack uses — see\n// docker-compose.infra.yml line 12. Pinning by digest guarantees the\n// init-script behaviour we're proving here matches what runs in\n// production. Bumping the digest is a deliberate review surface.\nconst POSTGRES_IMAGE =\n  'timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16@sha256:b3d038d0a0757df8a5ec0a94ba68d9ad57b0e16100a024cf4b370c77ad5645f7';\n\nconst DATABASE_NAME = 'aquaculture';\nconst DATABASE_USER = 'aquaculture';\nconst DATABASE_PASSWORD = 'aquaculture-test';\nconst DB_MIGRATE_DDL_AUTHORITY_ENV = 'DB_MIGRATE_DDL_AUTHORITY';\nconst SERVICE_ROLE_PASSWORD_ENVS = [\n  'AUTH_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'FARM_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'SENSOR_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'BILLING_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'HR_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'ALERT_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'ADMIN_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'GATEWAY_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'NOTIFICATION_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'HYDROPONICS_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'AI_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'MESSAGING_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'OBSERVABILITY_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'EVENT_STORE_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n  'CONFIG_SERVICE_DB_PASS',\n] as const;\n\n// Repo root, derived from this file's location:\n//   apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts -> ../../../..\n// (mirrors how the sibling platform-bootstrap.integration.spec.ts resolves it).\nconst REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..', '..', '..');\nconst INIT_SCRIPTS_DIR = join(REPO_ROOT, 'infrastructure', 'docker', 'init-scripts');\n\nasync function runWithDbMigrateDdlAuthority<T>(operation: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {\n  const previous = process.env[DB_MIGRATE_DDL_AUTHORITY_ENV];\n  process.env[DB_MIGRATE_DDL_AUTHORITY_ENV] = '1';\n  try {\n    return await operation();\n  } finally {\n    if (previous === undefined) {\n      Reflect.deleteProperty(process.env, DB_MIGRATE_DDL_AUTHORITY_ENV);\n    } else {\n      process.env[DB_MIGRATE_DDL_AUTHORITY_ENV] = previous;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Per-service migration manifest.\n *\n * Each entry maps a service to:\n *   - `schema`: the Postgres schema this service owns. The DataSource is\n *     bound to it so the migration ledger lands in `<schema>.migrations`.\n *   - `migrationsDir`: the on-disk path containing the service's\n *     timestamped TypeORM migration files. Two patterns exist in this","numLines":200,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1416}}}

validate_response(\n    envelope: dict[str, Any],\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    lease: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Validate an `aria/agent-response/v1` envelope.\n\n    `request` is optional — when provided, the response is cross-checked\n    against the request's `must_satisfy` ids and `expected_output_path`.\n\n    `lease` is optional — when provided (Plan 023 v3 §A-5), the\n    envelope's claim_id and agent_id MUST match the leased identity.\n    Pre-Plan-023 the envelope's claim_id was only validated for SHAPE\n    (CLAIM_ID_RE pattern) and the request_id was matched to the\n    request, but envelope.claim_id and envelope.agent_id were never\n    bound to the lease that authorized the submission. A stitched\n    envelope from a different lease (same request_id but different\n    claim/agent) would pass.\n    \"\"\"\n    missing = [f for f in RESPONSE_REQUIRED_FIELDS if f not in envelope]\n    if missing:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response missing required fields: {missing}\")\n    if envelope[\"$schema\"] != RESPONSE_SCHEMA:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response $schema must be {RESPONSE_SCHEMA!r}\")\n    if not isinstance(envelope[\"request_id\"], str) or not REQUEST_ID_RE.match(envelope[\"request_id\"]):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.request_id invalid: {envelope['request_id']!r}\")\n    claim_id = envelope[\"claim_id\"]\n    if not isinstance(claim_id, str) or not CLAIM_ID_RE.match(claim_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.claim_id invalid: {claim_id!r}\")\n    agent_id = envelope[\"agent_id\"]\n    if not isinstance(agent_id, str) or not AGENT_ID_RE.match(agent_id):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.agent_id invalid: {agent_id!r}\")\n    role = envelope[\"role\"]\n    if role not in INVOCATION_ROLES:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.role unknown: {role!r}\")\n    status = envelope[\"status\"]\n    if status not in RESPONSE_STATUSES:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"agent-response.status unknown: {status!r}\")\n\n    matrix = envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"]\n    if not isinstance(matrix, list):\n        raise GovernanceError(\"agent-response.satisfaction_matrix must be a list\")\n    matrix_entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    matrix_ids: set[str] = set()\n    for idx, entry in enumerate(matrix):\n        if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}] must be an object\")\n        item_id = entry.get(\"id\")\n        verdict = entry.get(\"verdict\")\n        if not isinstance(item_id, str) or not item_id.strip():\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id is required\")\n        if item_id in matrix_ids:\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].id duplicate: {item_id}\")\n        matrix_ids.add(item_id)\n        if verdict not in SATISFACTION_VERDICTS:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].verdict {verdict!r} not in {SATISFACTION_VERDICTS}\"\n            )\n        if verdict in {\"blocked\", \"contradicted\"}:\n            note = entry.get(\"note\") or entry.get(\"rationale\") or \"\"\n            if not isinstance(note, str) or not note.strip():\n                raise GovernanceError(\n                    f\"satisfaction_matrix[{idx}].note required when verdict={verdict!r}\"\n                )\n            _check_banned_phrases(note, field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].note\")\n            evidence_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\", [])\n            _ensure_string_list(\n                evidence_refs,\n                field=f\"satisfaction_matrix[{item_id}].evidence_refs\",\n                allow_empty=False,\n            )\n        matrix_entries.append(entry)\n\n    rationale = envelope.get(\"rationale\")\n    if isinstance(rationale, str) and rationale.strip():\n        _check_banned_phrases(rationale, field=\"agent-response.rationale\")\n\n    # Plan 023 v3 §A-5 — envelope claim/agent identity bound to lease.\n    # When the caller supplies the leased claim+agent identity (which\n    # submit_claim_result does), reject any envelope whose claim_id or\n    # agent_id differs. Pre-fix a stitched envelope from a different\n    # lease (same request_id but different claim/agent) passed because\n    # only request_id was cross-checked.\n    if lease is not None:\n        leased_claim = lease.get(\"claim_id\")\n        leased_agent = lease.get(\"agent_id\")\n        if leased_claim is not None and envelope[\"claim_id\"] != leased_claim:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"envelope_claim_id_mismatch: envelope.claim_id=\"\n                f\"{envelope['claim_id']!r} does not match leased claim_id=\"\n                f\"{leased_claim!r}\"\n            )\n        if leased_agent is not None and envelope[\"agent_id\"] != leased_agent:\n            raise GovernanceError(\n                f\"envelope_agent_id_mismatch: envelope.agent_id=\"\n                f\"{envelope['agent_id']!r} does not match leased agent_id=\"\n                f\"{leased_agent!r}\"\n            )\n","numLines":100,"startLine":363,"totalLines":616}}}

_accepted_native_runtime_result(\n    *, tools_dir: Path, request_id: str, claim_id: str, agent_id: str,\n    session_id: str, policy_digest: str,\n) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Join native acceptance to the sealed output and original attempt rows.\"\"\"\n    from aria_kernel import agent_invocations as invocations\n    from aria_kernel.ledger import load_declared_jsonl\n    from aria_kernel.tool_registry import GovernanceError\n\n    requests = invocations.list_agent_invocation_requests(request_id=request_id, base_dir=tools_dir)\n    if len(requests) != 1:\n        raise GovernanceError(\"native_runtime_result_request_unavailable\")\n    request = requests[0]\n    accepted = invocations.accepted_result_for_request(\n        request_id=request_id, role=request[\"role\"], base_dir=tools_dir,\n    )\n    if accepted is None or accepted.get(\"claim_id\") != claim_id or accepted.get(\"agent_id\") != agent_id:\n        raise GovernanceError(\"native_runtime_result_acceptance_unavailable\")\n    if any(accepted.get(name) != request.get(name) for name in (\"target_sha\", \"context_hash\", \"prompt_hash\")):\n        raise GovernanceError(\"native_runtime_result_request_evidence_unavailable\")\n    sealed_path = invocations.resolve_output_artifact_path(tools_dir, accepted[\"output_path\"])\n    invocations._assert_submission_artifact_path_safe(tools_dir, sealed_path)\n    content = invocations._read_stable_submission_artifact(sealed_path)\n    digest = \"sha256:\" + hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()\n    if digest != accepted.get(\"output_hash\") or digest != accepted.get(\"content_hash\"):\n        raise GovernanceError(\"native_runtime_result_sealed_hash_unavailable\")\n    envelope = json.loads(content)\n    if (not isinstance(envelope, dict) or envelope.get(\"request_id\") != request_id\n            or envelope.get(\"claim_id\") != claim_id or envelope.get(\"agent_id\") != agent_id\n            or envelope.get(\"role\") != request[\"role\"] or not isinstance(envelope.get(\"details\"), dict)):\n        raise GovernanceError(\"native_runtime_result_envelope_binding_unavailable\")\n    claims = load_declared_jsonl(tools_dir / \"agent-invocations/claims.jsonl\",\n                                expected_surface=\"agent_invocation_claims\")\n    original_claims = [row for row in claims if row.get(\"event\") == \"claimed\"\n                       and row.get(\"claim_id\") == claim_id and row.get(\"agent_id\") == agent_id\n                       and row.get(\"request_id\") == request_id]\n    if len(original_claims) != 1:\n        raise GovernanceError(\"native_runtime_result_claim_unavailable\")\n    governance = load_declared_jsonl(tools_dir / \"governance.jsonl\", expected_surface=\"tools_governance\")\n    attempts = [row for row in governance if row.get(\"kind\") == \"runtime_attempt_started\"\n                and row.get(\"ledger_hash\") == envelope[\"details\"].get(\"runtime_attempt_ledger_hash\")]\n    if len(attempts) != 1:\n        raise GovernanceError(\"native_runtime_result_attempt_unavailable\")\n    attempt = attempts[0]\n    required = {\"request_id\": request_id, \"request_ledger_hash\": request[\"ledger_hash\"],\n                \"claim_id\": claim_id, \"claim_ledger_hash\": original_claims[0][\"ledger_hash\"],\n                \"agent_id\": agent_id, \"session_id\": session_id, \"policy_digest\": policy_digest}\n    if any(attempt[\"details\"].get(name) != value for name, value in required.items()):\n        raise GovernanceError(\"native_runtime_result_attempt_binding_unavailable\")\n    return accepted, attempt\n\n\ndef _native_task_binding_refusal(*, repo_root: Path, tools_dir: Path, request: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Observe the actual checkout through existing binding and Git owners.\n\n    None when the checkout IS the request's task root at its anchor;\n    otherwise the named reason (``task_root_binding_unavailable`` /\n    ``target_revision_unavailable`` / ``target_revision_mismatch``), recorded\n    as a governance row and carried into the child's summary by the caller.\n    A worktree of the bound repository passes the identity check (the\n    binding compares git common directories), which is what lets the drain\n    serve each request in a worktree at its own anchor.\n","numLines":70,"startLine":3480,"totalLines":5532}}}
