{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NXV5gAy8s4zEnkHNzkEWW1","type":"tool_result","content":"5090\t        sys.stderr.write(f\"{DELIVERY_WINDOW_REFUSAL.release_reason}: {_publication_window_refusal} (before the publication)\\n\")\n5091\t        _release_claim(\n5092\t            tools_dir=tools_dir, repo=repo, claim_id=claim_id,\n5093\t            agent_id=agent_id, lease_token=lease_token,\n5094\t            reason=DELIVERY_WINDOW_REFUSAL.release_reason,\n5095\t        )\n5096\t        return _refuse_dispatch(\n5097\t            request=request_envelope, request_id=request_id, target_agent=subagent_type,\n5098\t            reason=DELIVERY_WINDOW_REFUSAL.release_reason,\n5099\t            failure_class=DELIVERY_WINDOW_REFUSAL.failure_class,\n5100\t            retryable=DELIVERY_WINDOW_REFUSAL.retryable,\n5101\t        )\n5102\t\n5103\t    # Plan ARIA-V8.13 — agent refusal as first-class terminal outcome.\n5104\t    # When the agent emits `aria/agent-refusal/v1` (legitimate refusal\n5105\t    # for insufficient evidence, scope conflict, content_hash mismatch,\n5106\t    # etc.), pre-V8.13 ci_executor treated the refusal envelope as a\n5107\t    # normal submit attempt: the canonical schema check failed\n5108\t    # (`plan_content:absent_or_not_object`), the consumer requeued,\n5109\t    # the agent refused again, and after N retries the request landed\n5110\t    # in HUMAN_REQUIRED — burning ~3× $0.35 Opus tokens per refusal.\n5111\t    #\n5112\t    # V8.13 detects the refusal envelope in agent_text + dispatches\n5113\t    # `aria_kernel human-required record` immediately, releases the\n5114\t    # claim with `reason=agent_refused:<class>`, and returns 0 so the\n5115\t    # consumer does NOT retry. The kernel state machine recognizes\n5116\t    # the human_required event as terminal (line 596 of\n5117\t    # agent_invocations.py). The drainer's poll observes no state\n5118\t    # transition and times out as usual — verdict=challenger_unavailable\n5119\t    # — but Opus cost stays at 1× per refusal instead of N×.\n5120\t    try:\n5121\t        _envelope_for_validation = json.loads(expected_output_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n5122\t    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as _exc:\n5123\t        _envelope_for_validation = None\n5124\t    # Refusal detection: look at the agent's raw text body, NOT the\n5125\t    # ci_executor-built outer wrapper. The agent's refusal JSON is\n5126\t    # nested inside `details.agent_text`. We parse the embedded JSON\n5127\t    # block ourselves to spot the `$schema = aria/agent-refusal/v1`\n5128\t    # marker independently of the outer envelope's claimed schema.\n5129\t    if isinstance(_envelope_for_validation, dict):\n5130\t        _agent_text = (_envelope_for_validation.get(\"details\") or {}).get(\"agent_text\") or \"\"\n5131\t        _inner_refusal = _extract_envelope_json(_agent_text) if isinstance(_agent_text, str) else None\n5132\t        if isinstance(_inner_refusal, dict) and (\n5133\t            _inner_refusal.get(\"$schema\") == \"aria/agent-refusal/v1\"\n5134\t            or _inner_refusal.get(\"envelope\") == \"aria/agent-refusal/v1\"\n5135\t            or _inner_refusal.get(\"schema\") == \"aria/agent-refusal/v1\"\n5136\t        ):\n5137\t            _reason_class = str(_inner_refusal.get(\"reason_class\") or \"unspecified\")\n5138\t            _reason_summary = str(\n5139\t                _inner_refusal.get(\"reason_summary\")\n5140\t                or _inner_refusal.get(\"reason\")\n5141\t                or \"agent refused without summary\"\n5142\t            )[:500]\n5143\t            _stage(f\"agent_refusal_detected class={_reason_class!r} request_id={request_id}\")\n5144\t            # Persist HUMAN_REQUIRED through the kernel's recorder (one\n5145\t            # recorder, shared with the delivery refusals — ARIA-HIGH-124)\n5146\t            # so the operator sees the structured triage row and the state\n5147\t            # machine marks the request terminal from the release below.\n5148\t            # The agent's own summary is free text of the agent's: it rides\n5149\t            # the record's structured context, never the reason the\n5150\t            # notification channels carry (data minimisation, the CLI\n5151\t            # validator's purpose, kept without the validator).\n5152\t            try:\n5153\t                _record_human_required(\n5154\t                    tools_dir=tools_dir, request_id=request_id, severity=\"MEDIUM\",\n5155\t                    reason=f\"agent_refused:{_reason_class}: the agent refused the request; its summary is on the record\",\n5156\t                    context={\"code\": f\"agent_refused:{_reason_class}\", \"stage\": \"agent_refusal\",\n5157\t                             \"claim_id\": claim_id, \"reason_class\": _reason_class, \"reason_summary\": _reason_summary},\n5158\t                )\n5159\t            except HumanRequiredRecordUnavailable as record_error:\n5160\t                return _release_unescalated(\n5161\t                    tools_dir=tools_dir, repo=repo, request=request_envelope, request_id=request_id,\n5162\t                    target_agent=subagent_type, claim_id=claim_id, agent_id=agent_id, lease_token=lease_token,\n5163\t                    escalation_reason=f\"agent_refused:{_reason_class}\", phase=\"submit\", error=record_error,\n5164\t                )\n5165\t            # Release the claim so downstream observers see the\n5166\t            # explicit `agent_refused:<class>` reason rather than the\n5167\t            # generic `plan_content_invalid` rejection that pre-V8.13\n5168\t            # surfaced. The release_claim helper also takes care of\n5169\t            # lease-token discipline + governance attribution.\n5170\t            _release_claim(\n5171\t                tools_dir=tools_dir, repo=repo, claim_id=claim_id,\n5172\t                agent_id=agent_id, lease_token=lease_token,\n5173\t                reason=f\"agent_refused:{_reason_class}\",\n5174\t            )\n5175\t            # Refusal is a legitimate terminal — not a build failure, and not\n5176\t            # a success either: invoke_claude_cli's summary said \"succeeded\"\n5177\t            # (the CLI ran to completion); this later terminal supersedes it\n5178\t            # so the drain never counts a refused envelope as drained. The\n5179\t            # agent-supplied class stays out of the summary (it is sanitized\n5180\t            # by construction); the release reason and the HUMAN_REQUIRED\n5181\t            # row carry it.\n5182\t            return _refuse_dispatch(\n5183\t                request=request_envelope, request_id=request_id, target_agent=subagent_type,\n5184\t                reason=\"agent_refused\",\n5185\t            )\n5186\t    if isinstance(_envelope_for_validation, dict):\n5187\t        # Plan ARIA-V8.4 — auto-fill missing canonical plan_content\n5188\t        # fields from compatible sources within the envelope before\n5189\t        # validation runs. The agent's substantive output stays\n5190\t        # untouched; only bookkeeping fields the agent dropped get\n5191\t        # populated (e.g. evidence_refs copied from top-level when\n5192\t        # plan_content omitted it). The normalizer never fabricates\n5193\t        # evidence — it only mirrors values already present.\n5194\t        _mutated = _canonicalize_plan_content(_envelope_for_validation)\n5195\t        # V8.7 + V8.19 — same canonicalization pattern for cross_review.\n5196\t        # V8.19: pass request_envelope so reviewer_agent fallback uses\n5197\t        # request.target_agent (kernel-trustworthy \"aria-cross-reviewer\")\n5198\t        # instead of the outer envelope's executor identity.\n5199\t        _mutated_cr = _canonicalize_cross_review("}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ff92a1d8-3185-463a-9cf3-02f1306689af","uuid":"db8e398e-42bc-4192-bf6a-9bc845b0f01c","timestamp":"2026-09-21T02:18:12.039Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-d333052f1946/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"        sys.stderr.write(f\"{DELIVERY_WINDOW_REFUSAL.release_reason}: {_publication_window_refusal} (before the publication)\\n\")\n        _release_claim(\n            tools_dir=tools_dir, repo=repo, claim_id=claim_id,\n            agent_id=agent_id, lease_token=lease_token,\n            reason=DELIVERY_WINDOW_REFUSAL.release_reason,\n        )\n        return _refuse_dispatch(\n            request=request_envelope, request_id=request_id, target_agent=subagent_type,\n            reason=DELIVERY_WINDOW_REFUSAL.release_reason,\n            failure_class=DELIVERY_WINDOW_REFUSAL.failure_class,\n            retryable=DELIVERY_WINDOW_REFUSAL.retryable,\n        )\n\n    # Plan ARIA-V8.13 — agent refusal as first-class terminal outcome.\n    # When the agent emits `aria/agent-refusal/v1` (legitimate refusal\n    # for insufficient evidence, scope conflict, content_hash mismatch,\n    # etc.), pre-V8.13 ci_executor treated the refusal envelope as a\n    # normal submit attempt: the canonical schema check failed\n    # (`plan_content:absent_or_not_object`), the consumer requeued,\n    # the agent refused again, and after N retries the request landed\n    # in HUMAN_REQUIRED — burning ~3× $0.35 Opus tokens per refusal.\n    #\n    # V8.13 detects the refusal envelope in agent_text + dispatches\n    # `aria_kernel human-required record` immediately, releases the\n    # claim with `reason=agent_refused:<class>`, and returns 0 so the\n    # consumer does NOT retry. The kernel state machine recognizes\n    # the human_required event as terminal (line 596 of\n    # agent_invocations.py). The drainer's poll observes no state\n    # transition and times out as usual — verdict=challenger_unavailable\n    # — but Opus cost stays at 1× per refusal instead of N×.\n    try:\n        _envelope_for_validation = json.loads(expected_output_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as _exc:\n        _envelope_for_validation = None\n    # Refusal detection: look at the agent's raw text body, NOT the\n    # ci_executor-built outer wrapper. The agent's refusal JSON is\n    # nested inside `details.agent_text`. We parse the embedded JSON\n    # block ourselves to spot the `$schema = aria/agent-refusal/v1`\n    # marker independently of the outer envelope's claimed schema.\n    if isinstance(_envelope_for_validation, dict):\n        _agent_text = (_envelope_for_validation.get(\"details\") or {}).get(\"agent_text\") or \"\"\n        _inner_refusal = _extract_envelope_json(_agent_text) if isinstance(_agent_text, str) else None\n        if isinstance(_inner_refusal, dict) and (\n            _inner_refusal.get(\"$schema\") == \"aria/agent-refusal/v1\"\n            or _inner_refusal.get(\"envelope\") == \"aria/agent-refusal/v1\"\n            or _inner_refusal.get(\"schema\") == \"aria/agent-refusal/v1\"\n        ):\n            _reason_class = str(_inner_refusal.get(\"reason_class\") or \"unspecified\")\n            _reason_summary = str(\n                _inner_refusal.get(\"reason_summary\")\n                or _inner_refusal.get(\"reason\")\n                or \"agent refused without summary\"\n            )[:500]\n            _stage(f\"agent_refusal_detected class={_reason_class!r} request_id={request_id}\")\n            # Persist HUMAN_REQUIRED through the kernel's recorder (one\n            # recorder, shared with the delivery refusals — ARIA-HIGH-124)\n            # so the operator sees the structured triage row and the state\n            # machine marks the request terminal from the release below.\n            # The agent's own summary is free text of the agent's: it rides\n            # the record's structured context, never the reason the\n            # notification channels carry (data minimisation, the CLI\n            # validator's purpose, kept without the validator).\n            try:\n                _record_human_required(\n                    tools_dir=tools_dir, request_id=request_id, severity=\"MEDIUM\",\n                    reason=f\"agent_refused:{_reason_class}: the agent refused the request; its summary is on the record\",\n                    context={\"code\": f\"agent_refused:{_reason_class}\", \"stage\": \"agent_refusal\",\n                             \"claim_id\": claim_id, \"reason_class\": _reason_class, \"reason_summary\": _reason_summary},\n                )\n            except HumanRequiredRecordUnavailable as record_error:\n                return _release_unescalated(\n                    tools_dir=tools_dir, repo=repo, request=request_envelope, request_id=request_id,\n                    target_agent=subagent_type, claim_id=claim_id, agent_id=agent_id, lease_token=lease_token,\n                    escalation_reason=f\"agent_refused:{_reason_class}\", phase=\"submit\", error=record_error,\n                )\n            # Release the claim so downstream observers see the\n            # explicit `agent_refused:<class>` reason rather than the\n            # generic `plan_content_invalid` rejection that pre-V8.13\n            # surfaced. The release_claim helper also takes care of\n            # lease-token discipline + governance attribution.\n            _release_claim(\n                tools_dir=tools_dir, repo=repo, claim_id=claim_id,\n                agent_id=agent_id, lease_token=lease_token,\n                reason=f\"agent_refused:{_reason_class}\",\n            )\n            # Refusal is a legitimate terminal — not a build failure, and not\n            # a success either: invoke_claude_cli's summary said \"succeeded\"\n            # (the CLI ran to completion); this later terminal supersedes it\n            # so the drain never counts a refused envelope as drained. The\n            # agent-supplied class stays out of the summary (it is sanitized\n            # by construction); the release reason and the HUMAN_REQUIRED\n            # row carry it.\n            return _refuse_dispatch(\n                request=request_envelope, request_id=request_id, target_agent=subagent_type,\n                reason=\"agent_refused\",\n            )\n    if isinstance(_envelope_for_validation, dict):\n        # Plan ARIA-V8.4 — auto-fill missing canonical plan_content\n        # fields from compatible sources within the envelope before\n        # validation runs. The agent's substantive output stays\n        # untouched; only bookkeeping fields the agent dropped get\n        # populated (e.g. evidence_refs copied from top-level when\n        # plan_content omitted it). The normalizer never fabricates\n        # evidence — it only mirrors values already present.\n        _mutated = _canonicalize_plan_content(_envelope_for_validation)\n        # V8.7 + V8.19 — same canonicalization pattern for cross_review.\n        # V8.19: pass request_envelope so reviewer_agent fallback uses\n        # request.target_agent (kernel-trustworthy \"aria-cross-reviewer\")\n        # instead of the outer envelope's executor identity.\n        _mutated_cr = _canonicalize_cross_review(","numLines":110,"startLine":5090,"totalLines":5532}}}

   ARIA-HIGH-022 — \"auto\": the kernel CLI resolves the workspace HEAD and\n    grounds the evidence check there when it has moved past the request\n    base — one flag, no extra subprocess on this side (the fused-envelope\n    smoke contract counts subprocess calls).\n    \"\"\"\n    submit_argv = _kernel_cli_argv(\n        \"agent\", \"submit-result\",\n        \"--claim-id\", claim_id,\n        \"--agent-id\", agent_id,\n        \"--lease-token-from-env\", LEASE_TOKEN_ENV_VAR,\n        \"--output-path\", str(expected_output_path),\n        \"--workspace-root\", str(repo),\n        \"--tools-dir\", str(tools_dir),\n        \"--context-hash\", str(request_envelope.get(\"context_hash\") or \"\"),\n        \"--prompt-hash\", str(request_envelope.get(\"prompt_hash\") or \"\"),\n        \"--transcript-hash\", transcript_hash,\n        \"--transcript-artifact-ref\", transcript_output_path.resolve().as_posix(),\n    )\n    submit_argv += [\"--evidence-target-sha\", \"auto\"]\n    return subprocess.run(\n        submit_argv,\n        capture_output=True,\n        text=True,\n        env={\n            **os.environ,\n            \"PYTHONPATH\": _KERNEL_PYTHONPATH,\n            LEASE_TOKEN_ENV_VAR: lease_token,\n        },\n        timeout=SUBMIT_RESULT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,\n    )\n\n\ndef _reconcile_native_result(\n    *, tools_dir: Path, request_id: str, claim_id: str, agent_id: str, session_id: str | None,\n    policy_digest: str, request_envelope: dict[str, Any],\n) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"After a native-route submit: prove the accepted row is this attempt's\n    and record `runtime_attempt_reconciled`. False after the unavailability\n    was written to stderr (the caller exits 1).\"\"\"\n    from aria_kernel.tool_registry import GovernanceError, append_tools_governance\n\n    try:\n        accepted, attempt = _accepted_native_runtime_result(\n            tools_dir=tools_dir, request_id=request_id, claim_id=claim_id, agent_id=agent_id,\n            session_id=session_id, policy_digest=policy_digest,\n        )\n    except (GovernanceError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:\n        sys.stderr.write(\"native_runtime_result_reconciliation_unavailable:\" + type(exc).__name__ + \"\\n\")\n        return False\n    append_tools_governance(tools_dir, \"runtime_attempt_reconciled\", {\n        \"schema_version\": 1, \"request_id\": request_id, \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n        \"request_ledger_hash\": request_envelope[\"request_ledger_hash\"],\n        \"claim_ledger_hash\": request_envelope[\"claim_ledger_hash\"],\n        \"attempt_ledger_hash\": attempt[\"ledger_hash\"],\n        \"result_ledger_hash\": accepted[\"ledger_hash\"], \"result_status\": accepted[\"status\"],\n        \"agent_id\": agent_id, \"session_id\": session_id,\n        \"policy_digest\": policy_digest,\n    })\n    return True\n\n\ndef _main(argv: list[str] | None, *, _runtime_stack: _ExitStack) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Entry point — runs one cycle. Designed to be called by GHA step.\"\"\"\n    global _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY\n    args = argv if argv is not None else sys.argv[1:]\n    if len(args) < 1:\n        print(\n            \"usage: ci_executor.py <request_id> [subagent_type] | --drain | \"\n            \"--judge-batch <role> <target_agent> <request_id>...\",\n            file=sys.stderr,\n        )\n        return 2\n\n    if args[0] == \"--judge-batch\":\n        # Typed-judgment plan Phase 4b (ARIA-MEDIUM-163) — K judge requests\n        # that share a role, an agent and an anchor, served by ONE typed\n        # model call. The drain selected them; this child claims, asks,\n        # seals and submits each one through the same seams as the\n        # single-request path (`ci_executor_judge_batch`).\n        if len(args) < 4:\n            print(\"usage: ci_executor.py --judge-batch <role> <target_agent> <request_id>...\", file=sys.stderr)\n            return 2\n        from ci_executor_judge_batch import run_judge_batch\n\n        _MOCK_MODE_AT_ENTRY = _is_mock_mode()\n        batch_repo = Path.cwd().resolve()\n        batch_env_tools = os.environ.get(\"ARIA_TOOLS_DIR\")\n        batch_tools_dir = Path(batch_env_tools).resolve() if batch_env_tools else batch_repo / \"aria-tools\"\n        _record_mock_mode_audit(batch_tools_dir)","numLines":90,"startLine":4180,"totalLines":5532}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016BHnZYWpRDD5zswZeCm97w","type":"tool_result","content":"2675-    if \"evidence_refs\" not in envelope:\n2676-        envelope[\"evidence_refs\"] = []\n2677-\n2678-    # Embed the raw agent text under details so operators have full\n2679-    # forensic context post-submission.\n2680-    details = envelope.get(\"details\")\n2681-    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n2682-        details = {}\n2683-    # D1 (Kapalı Döngü) — FORCE-set, not setdefault: this field is now the\n2684-    # judge's calibration identity (judgment_bridge reads it as judge_id),\n2685-    # and a setdefault would let the agent's own payload spoof another\n2686-    # judge's identity into the per-judge precision ledger.\n2687-    details[\"agent_subagent_type\"] = subagent_type\n2688-    # ORPHAN-HIGH-781 — the dispatch-resolved model, same doctrine as\n2689-    # agent_subagent_type above: FORCE-set from the runtime profile the\n2690-    # executor resolved at invocation time. The judge's own\n2691-    # verdict.model self-report remains in the payload as data, but the\n2692-    # kernel's anchor distinct-model count must not trust a string the\n2693-    # judged agent wrote about itself — a misreported model silently\n2694-    # satisfies or violates ANCHOR_MIN_DISTINCT_MODELS, which is exactly\n2695-    # the guarantee that field exists to provide.\n2696-    # ARIA-MEDIUM-171 — the route's word, never the agent's: a named model\n2697-    # overwrites whatever the agent wrote under the key, and a route that\n2698-    # named nothing REMOVES the key rather than leaving the agent's spelling\n2699-    # in place (ORPHAN-HIGH-781 pinned \"no stale stamp\" for the absent case).\n2700-    if dispatch_model:\n2701-        details[\"agent_dispatch_model\"] = dispatch_model\n2702-    else:\n2703-        details.pop(\"agent_dispatch_model\", None)\n2704-    details[\"agent_confidence_source\"] = confidence_source\n2705:    details.setdefault(\"agent_text\", _safe_agent_text_excerpt(agent_text))\n2706-    if usage is None:\n2707-        usage = extract_usage(parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout))\n2708-    if usage is not None:\n2709-        details.setdefault(\"claude_cli_usage\", usage)\n2710-    envelope[\"details\"] = details\n2711-\n2712-    return envelope\n2713-\n--\n5100-            retryable=DELIVERY_WINDOW_REFUSAL.retryable,\n5101-        )\n5102-\n5103-    # Plan ARIA-V8.13 — agent refusal as first-class terminal outcome.\n5104-    # When the agent emits `aria/agent-refusal/v1` (legitimate refusal\n5105-    # for insufficient evidence, scope conflict, content_hash mismatch,\n5106-    # etc.), pre-V8.13 ci_executor treated the refusal envelope as a\n5107-    # normal submit attempt: the canonical schema check failed\n5108-    # (`plan_content:absent_or_not_object`), the consumer requeued,\n5109-    # the agent refused again, and after N retries the request landed\n5110-    # in HUMAN_REQUIRED — burning ~3× $0.35 Opus tokens per refusal.\n5111-    #\n5112-    # V8.13 detects the refusal envelope in agent_text + dispatches\n5113-    # {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KTBVGR55BmiTepW43UnrLn","type":"tool_result","content":"2590\t    role: str,\n2591\t    subagent_type: str,\n2592\t    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n2593\t    dispatch_model: str | None = None,\n2594\t    usage: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n2595\t    confidence_source: str = \"self_reported\",\n2596\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n2597\t    \"\"\"Convert ``claude -p stream-json`` JSONL into a kernel-valid envelope.\n2598\t\n2599\t    ``confidence_source`` is the ROUTE's word on where a judge's confidence\n2600\t    came from (typed-judgment plan, ARIA-HIGH-167): every CLI and chat\n2601\t    completion in the fleet reports the model's own number\n2602\t    (``self_reported``); a typed decision transport that returns\n2603\t    probabilities natively stamps ``provider_reported``. Stamped\n2604\t    unconditionally, like the dispatch model: the bridge reads the stamp and\n2605\t    ignores any spelling the model wrote inside its payload.\n2606\t\n2607\t    ``usage`` is the run result's own usage block when the caller holds one\n2608\t    (ARIA-HIGH-161): the Z.ai transport reports usage on the result, not as\n2609\t    a stream-json event, so parsing ``raw_stdout`` alone left the\n2610\t    cross-vendor rung's envelope without ``claude_cli_usage`` and the native\n2611\t    wrapper released a finished verdict as ``usage_unavailable``. The stream\n2612\t    parse stays the fallback for a caller that has only the bytes.\n2613\t\n2614\t    Claude Code emits JSONL events. ARIA keeps those raw events out of\n2615\t    artifacts, extracts the final agent message, and injects the\n2616\t    lease-bound identity fields that the agent cannot know.\n2617\t    \"\"\"\n2618\t    events = parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout)\n2619\t    agent_text = extract_final_message(events)\n2620\t    if not agent_text:\n2621\t        agent_text = raw_stdout\n2622\t    extracted = _extract_envelope_json(agent_text) or {}\n2623\t\n2624\t    envelope: dict[str, Any] = {\n2625\t        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n2626\t        \"request_id\": request_id,\n2627\t        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n2628\t        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n2629\t        \"role\": role,\n2630\t        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n2631\t    }\n2632\t\n2633\t    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n2634\t    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n2635\t        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n2636\t    else:\n2637\t        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n2638\t        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). The agent\n2639\t        # being invoked and replying is transport evidence, not a\n2640\t        # satisfaction judgment; the verdict belongs to an independent\n2641\t        # judge reading this matrix, never to the executor that carried\n2642\t        # the reply.\n2643\t        synthesized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n2644\t        excerpt = (agent_text or \"<agent produced no text>\").strip()\n2645\t        excerpt_short = excerpt[:240] + (\"...\" if len(excerpt) > 240 else \"\")\n2646\t        if must_satisfy:\n2647\t            for item in must_satisfy:\n2648\t                if not isinstance(item, dict):\n2649\t                    continue\n2650\t                cid = item.get(\"id\")\n2651\t                if not cid:\n2652\t                    continue\n2653\t                synthesized.append({\n2654\t                    \"id\": cid,\n2655\t                    \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n2656\t                    \"evidence_refs\": [],\n2657\t                    \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n2658\t                })\n2659\t        if not synthesized:\n2660\t            # Last-resort single-row matrix; agent_text is the only\n2661\t            # truthful evidence. Without this, the kernel rejects with\n2662\t            # evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty.\n2663\t            synthesized.append({\n2664\t                \"id\": f\"agent-text-{request_id[-8:]}\",\n2665\t                \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n2666\t                \"evidence_refs\": [],\n2667\t                \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n2668\t            })\n2669\t        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = synthesized\n2670\t\n2671\t    # Carry through any agent-supplied evidence_refs / details / notes.\n2672\t    for passthrough in (\"evidence_refs\", \"details\", \"notes\", \"plan_content\"):\n2673\t        if passthrough in extracted and extracted[passthrough] is not None:\n2674\t            envelope[passthrough] = extracted[passthrough]\n2675\t    if \"evidence_refs\" not in envelope:\n2676\t        envelope[\"evidence_refs\"] = []\n2677\t\n2678\t    # Embed the raw agent text under details so operators have full\n2679\t    # forensic context post-submission."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ff92a1d8-3185-463a-9cf3-02f1306689af","uuid":"b90b1a33-618b-4a6f-9eb7-9db37f84c5a4","timestamp":"2026-09-21T02:18:25.983Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-d333052f1946/tools/aria-poc/ci_executor.py","content":"    role: str,\n    subagent_type: str,\n    must_satisfy: list[dict[str, Any]],\n    dispatch_model: str | None = None,\n    usage: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n    confidence_source: str = \"self_reported\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Convert ``claude -p stream-json`` JSONL into a kernel-valid envelope.\n\n    ``confidence_source`` is the ROUTE's word on where a judge's confidence\n    came from (typed-judgment plan, ARIA-HIGH-167): every CLI and chat\n    completion in the fleet reports the model's own number\n    (``self_reported``); a typed decision transport that returns\n    probabilities natively stamps ``provider_reported``. Stamped\n    unconditionally, like the dispatch model: the bridge reads the stamp and\n    ignores any spelling the model wrote inside its payload.\n\n    ``usage`` is the run result's own usage block when the caller holds one\n    (ARIA-HIGH-161): the Z.ai transport reports usage on the result, not as\n    a stream-json event, so parsing ``raw_stdout`` alone left the\n    cross-vendor rung's envelope without ``claude_cli_usage`` and the native\n    wrapper released a finished verdict as ``usage_unavailable``. The stream\n    parse stays the fallback for a caller that has only the bytes.\n\n    Claude Code emits JSONL events. ARIA keeps those raw events out of\n    artifacts, extracts the final agent message, and injects the\n    lease-bound identity fields that the agent cannot know.\n    \"\"\"\n    events = parse_claude_jsonl(raw_stdout)\n    agent_text = extract_final_message(events)\n    if not agent_text:\n        agent_text = raw_stdout\n    extracted = _extract_envelope_json(agent_text) or {}\n\n    envelope: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"$schema\": \"aria/agent-response/v1\",\n        \"request_id\": request_id,\n        \"claim_id\": claim_id,\n        \"agent_id\": agent_id,\n        \"role\": role,\n        \"status\": str(extracted.get(\"status\") or \"submitted\"),\n    }\n\n    matrix_in = extracted.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\")\n    if isinstance(matrix_in, list) and matrix_in:\n        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = matrix_in\n    else:\n        # Synthesize from must_satisfy so the kernel's non-empty-matrix\n        # check passes — as UNVERIFIED rows (ARIA-AUDIT-024). The agent\n        # being invoked and replying is transport evidence, not a\n        # satisfaction judgment; the verdict belongs to an independent\n        # judge reading this matrix, never to the executor that carried\n        # the reply.\n        synthesized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        excerpt = (agent_text or \"<agent produced no text>\").strip()\n        excerpt_short = excerpt[:240] + (\"...\" if len(excerpt) > 240 else \"\")\n        if must_satisfy:\n            for item in must_satisfy:\n                if not isinstance(item, dict):\n                    continue\n                cid = item.get(\"id\")\n                if not cid:\n                    continue\n                synthesized.append({\n                    \"id\": cid,\n                    \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n                    \"evidence_refs\": [],\n                    \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n                })\n        if not synthesized:\n            # Last-resort single-row matrix; agent_text is the only\n            # truthful evidence. Without this, the kernel rejects with\n            # evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty.\n            synthesized.append({\n                \"id\": f\"agent-text-{request_id[-8:]}\",\n                \"verdict\": \"unverified\",\n                \"evidence_refs\": [],\n                \"evidence\": excerpt_short,\n            })\n        envelope[\"satisfaction_matrix\"] = synthesized\n\n    # Carry through any agent-supplied evidence_refs / details / notes.\n    for passthrough in (\"evidence_refs\", \"details\", \"notes\", \"plan_content\"):\n        if passthrough in extracted and extracted[passthrough] is not None:\n            envelope[passthrough] = extracted[passthrough]\n    if \"evidence_refs\" not in envelope:\n        envelope[\"evidence_refs\"] = []\n\n    # Embed the raw agent text under details so operators have full\n    # forensic context post-submission.","numLines":90,"startLine":2590,"totalLines":5532}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Jzqb3u8vfC1qSZB8a7L8sV","type":"tool_result","content":"60\t\n61\t\n62\tdef _coerce_severity(value: Any, *, fallback: str = \"medium\") -> str:\n63\t    \"\"\"feedback_store accepts {low, medium, high, critical}; agent envelope\n64\t    may use uppercase per finding schema. Normalize without rejecting.\"\"\"\n65\t    if not isinstance(value, str):\n66\t        return fallback\n67\t    normalized = value.strip().lower()\n68\t    if normalized in FEEDBACK_SEVERITIES:\n69\t        return normalized\n70\t    if normalized == \"informational\":\n71\t        return \"low\"\n72\t    return fallback\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tdef _verdict_field(details: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n76\t    \"\"\"The judge .md prompts shape verdicts under details.verdict (evidence,\n77\t    adversarial) or details.consensus (arbiter). Return whichever exists.\n78\t    \"\"\"\n79\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), dict):\n80\t        return dict(details[\"verdict\"])\n81\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"consensus\"), dict):\n82\t        return dict(details[\"consensus\"])\n83\t    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), str):\n84\t        # Flat shape (ORPHAN-HIGH-629 sibling): the judge contract file says\n85\t        # BOTH \"Return JSON with: verdict, judge_id, confidence...\" and\n86\t        # \"details.verdict.rationale\". The first live judge run answered\n87\t        # flat, was ACCEPTED by the envelope gate, and dropped HERE with\n88\t        # \"expected verdict ..., got None\" — a verdict lost between two\n89\t        # halves of one document. Honour both spellings; the envelope gate\n90\t        # stays the only authority on validity.\n91\t        return {\n92\t            key: details.get(key)\n93\t            for key in (\n94\t                \"verdict\", \"tool_id\", \"run_id\", \"finding_id\", \"judge_id\",\n95\t                \"model\", \"prompt_hash\", \"confidence\", \"rationale\",\n96\t                \"evidence_refs\", \"judgment_group_id\", \"severity\",\n97\t            )\n98\t            if details.get(key) is not None\n99\t        }\n100\t    return {}\n101\t\n102\t\n103\tdef typed_verdict_block(verdict_block: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n104\t    \"\"\"The verdict block when it is a TYPED answer (typed-judgment plan).\n105\t\n106\t    A typed block names its ``primitive`` (``choice`` for the binary judge\n107\t    roles), carries ``value`` instead of ``verdict``, cites evidence by\n108\t    ``{index, quote}`` and never names a path. A legacy block names\n109\t    ``verdict`` and carries its own ``evidence_refs``; both remain valid, and\n110\t    each is validated by its own law.\n111\t    \"\"\"\n112\t    if verdict_block.get(\"primitive\") in PRIMITIVES:\n113\t        return dict(verdict_block)\n114\t    return None\n115\t\n116\t\n117\tdef _typed_question_for_request(request: dict[str, Any]) -> ChoiceQuestion:\n118\t    \"\"\"The request re-expressed as the choice the typed judge answered.\n119\t\n120\t    Options are the closed verdict vocabulary; refs and pinned excerpts are\n121\t    the request's own, so an index resolves to the request's ref and a quote\n122\t    is checked against the excerpt the mint pinned.\n123\t    \"\"\"\n124\t    refs = [str(ref) for ref in (request.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []) if isinstance(ref, str) and ref.strip()]\n125\t    excerpts = request.get(\"evidence_excerpts\")\n126\t    return ChoiceQuestion(\n127\t        question_id=str(request.get(\"request_id\") or \"request\"),\n128\t        prompt=str(request.get(\"suggested_prompt\") or \"judge the finding\"),\n129\t        options=tuple(FEEDBACK_VERDICTS),\n130\t        evidence_refs=tuple(refs),\n131\t        evidence_excerpts=tuple(excerpts) if isinstance(excerpts, list) else None,\n132\t    )\n133\t\n134\t\n135\tdef parse_typed_verdict(\n136\t    request: dict[str, Any], verdict_block: dict[str, Any],\n137\t) -> tuple[ChoiceQuestion, TypedAnswer | ParseFailure]:\n138\t    \"\"\"Parse a typed block against the request with the typed-judgment parser.\"\"\"\n139\t    question = _typed_question_for_request(request)\n140\t    payload = {**verdict_block, \"question_id\": question.question_id, \"primitive\": \"choice\"}\n141\t    return question, parse_single_answer(question, payload)\n142\t\n143\t\n144\tdef stamped_confidence_source(details: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, str | None]:\n145\t    \"\"\"The route's stamp for where the confidence came from, and why it is refused.\n146\t\n147\t    Returns ``(source, error)``. An envelope sealed before the stamp existed\n148\t    is a model's own number: ``self_reported``. ``provider_reported`` stands\n149\t    only when the stamped dispatch model belongs to a provider the fleet\n150\t    marks confidence-native (`model_fleet.provider_reports_confidence`); a\n151\t    spelling the model wrote inside its verdict block is never read.\n152\t    \"\"\"\n153\t    stamp = details.get(\"agent_confidence_source\")\n154\t    if stamp is None:\n155\t        return \"self_reported\", None\n156\t    if stamp not in CONFIDENCE_SOURCES:\n157\t        return \"self_reported\", f\"judge_verdict.confidence_source:invalid:{stamp!r}\"\n158\t    if stamp == \"provider_reported\" and not provider_reports_confidence(details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\")):\n159\t        return \"self_reported\", \"judge_verdict.confidence_source:unstamped\"\n160\t    return str(stamp), None\n161\t\n162\t\n163\tdef validate_judge_response(\n164\t    *,\n165\t    request: dict[str, Any],\n166\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n167\t) -> list[str]:\n168\t    \"\"\"Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — the judge output contract, checkable BEFORE accept.\n169\t\n170\t    The second sealed night accepted 12 judge results whose envelopes never\n171\t    carried a verdict the bridge could read (\"expected verdict ..., got\n172\t    None\") — each one an accepted row that could never fold, burning bridge\n173\t    retries toward permanent_fail. This is the SINGLE definition of what a\n174\t    judge response must carry; ``record_judge_verdict_from_response`` raises\n175\t    on exactly this list, and the executor's pre-submit gate releases the\n176\t    claim on it instead of sealing an unfoldable result.\n177\t\n178\t    Returns [] for non-judge roles: the contract binds judges only.\n179\t    \"\"\"\n180\t    role = response.get(\"role\")\n181\t    if not is_judge_role(role):\n182\t        return []\n183\t    errors: list[str] = []\n184\t    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n185\t    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n186\t        details = {}\n187\t    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n188\t    if not verdict_block:\n189\t        return [\"judge_verdict:absent\"]\n190\t    _source, source_error = stamped_confidence_source(details)\n191\t    if source_error:\n192\t        errors.append(source_error)\n193\t    typed = typed_verdict_block(verdict_block)\n194\t    if typed is not None:\n195\t        # Typed-judgment plan — the typed law is the parser's: primitive,\n196\t        # value in the closed vocabulary, confidence contract, index+quote\n197\t        # citations against the request's pinned excerpts, rationale.\n198\t        if typed.get(\"primitive\") != \"choice\":\n199\t            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.typed:primitive_mismatch:{typed.get('primitive')!r}\")\n200\t        else:\n201\t            try:\n202\t                _question, outcome = parse_typed_verdict(request, typed)\n203\t            except TypedJudgmentError as exc:\n204\t                outcome = ParseFailure(\"request\", \"evidence_missing\", str(exc))\n205\t            if isinstance(outcome, ParseFailure):\n206\t                errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.typed:{outcome.reason_code}\")\n207\t        verdict = typed.get(\"value\")\n208\t    else:\n209\t        verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n210\t    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS and typed is None:\n211\t        # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's contract says \"return\n212\t        # details.consensus, OR the uncertainty reason when the consensus\n213\t        # gate cannot be met\", and the deterministic engine it mirrors\n214\t        # emits exactly that non-verdict outcome. The live arbiter obeyed\n215\t        # and this contract burned the claim as invalid:None — fourth\n216\t        # mint-vs-law instance. Uncertainty is a VALID arbitration\n217\t        # outcome, for the arbiter alone, with a reason from the closed\n218\t        # vocabulary; binary judges stay bound to binary verdicts.\n219\t        reason = verdict_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n220\t        if (\n221\t            role == \"consensus_arbitration\"\n222\t            and verdict is None\n223\t            and reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS\n224\t        ):\n225\t            pass\n226\t        else:\n227\t            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.verdict:invalid:{verdict!r}\")\n228\t    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n229\t    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n230\t    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n231\t    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n232\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_tool_run_or_finding_id\")\n233\t    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n234\t    if not judge_id:\n235\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_judge_identity\")\n236\t    elif str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n237\t        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:executor_shaped_judge_identity\")\n238\t    return errors\n239\t\n240\t\n241\tdef record_judge_verdict_from_response(\n242\t    *,\n243\t    request: dict[str, Any],\n244\t    response: dict[str, Any],\n245\t    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n246\t) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n247\t    \"\"\"Translate an ACCEPTED judge response into an ai_judge feedback row.\n248\t\n249\t    Returns the persisted row, or None when the envelope role is not a\n250\t    judge role (the bridge is a no-op for non-judge submissions).\n251\t\n252\t    Raises GovernanceError when the response is missing the data the\n253\t    consensus engine requires (tool_id / run_id / finding_id come from\n254\t    the request envelope's optional fields; verdict + severity from\n255\t    the response.details).\n256\t    \"\"\"\n257\t    role = response.get(\"role\")\n258\t    if not is_judge_role(role):\n259\t        return None"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ff92a1d8-3185-463a-9cf3-02f1306689af","uuid":"e10fd53a-af27-4b49-8ce3-e517e723eeec","timestamp":"2026-09-21T02:18:45.040Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-d333052f1946/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/judgment_bridge.py","content":"\n\ndef _coerce_severity(value: Any, *, fallback: str = \"medium\") -> str:\n    \"\"\"feedback_store accepts {low, medium, high, critical}; agent envelope\n    may use uppercase per finding schema. Normalize without rejecting.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(value, str):\n        return fallback\n    normalized = value.strip().lower()\n    if normalized in FEEDBACK_SEVERITIES:\n        return normalized\n    if normalized == \"informational\":\n        return \"low\"\n    return fallback\n\n\ndef _verdict_field(details: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"The judge .md prompts shape verdicts under details.verdict (evidence,\n    adversarial) or details.consensus (arbiter). Return whichever exists.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), dict):\n        return dict(details[\"verdict\"])\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"consensus\"), dict):\n        return dict(details[\"consensus\"])\n    if isinstance(details.get(\"verdict\"), str):\n        # Flat shape (ORPHAN-HIGH-629 sibling): the judge contract file says\n        # BOTH \"Return JSON with: verdict, judge_id, confidence...\" and\n        # \"details.verdict.rationale\". The first live judge run answered\n        # flat, was ACCEPTED by the envelope gate, and dropped HERE with\n        # \"expected verdict ..., got None\" — a verdict lost between two\n        # halves of one document. Honour both spellings; the envelope gate\n        # stays the only authority on validity.\n        return {\n            key: details.get(key)\n            for key in (\n                \"verdict\", \"tool_id\", \"run_id\", \"finding_id\", \"judge_id\",\n                \"model\", \"prompt_hash\", \"confidence\", \"rationale\",\n                \"evidence_refs\", \"judgment_group_id\", \"severity\",\n            )\n            if details.get(key) is not None\n        }\n    return {}\n\n\ndef typed_verdict_block(verdict_block: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"The verdict block when it is a TYPED answer (typed-judgment plan).\n\n    A typed block names its ``primitive`` (``choice`` for the binary judge\n    roles), carries ``value`` instead of ``verdict``, cites evidence by\n    ``{index, quote}`` and never names a path. A legacy block names\n    ``verdict`` and carries its own ``evidence_refs``; both remain valid, and\n    each is validated by its own law.\n    \"\"\"\n    if verdict_block.get(\"primitive\") in PRIMITIVES:\n        return dict(verdict_block)\n    return None\n\n\ndef _typed_question_for_request(request: dict[str, Any]) -> ChoiceQuestion:\n    \"\"\"The request re-expressed as the choice the typed judge answered.\n\n    Options are the closed verdict vocabulary; refs and pinned excerpts are\n    the request's own, so an index resolves to the request's ref and a quote\n    is checked against the excerpt the mint pinned.\n    \"\"\"\n    refs = [str(ref) for ref in (request.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []) if isinstance(ref, str) and ref.strip()]\n    excerpts = request.get(\"evidence_excerpts\")\n    return ChoiceQuestion(\n        question_id=str(request.get(\"request_id\") or \"request\"),\n        prompt=str(request.get(\"suggested_prompt\") or \"judge the finding\"),\n        options=tuple(FEEDBACK_VERDICTS),\n        evidence_refs=tuple(refs),\n        evidence_excerpts=tuple(excerpts) if isinstance(excerpts, list) else None,\n    )\n\n\ndef parse_typed_verdict(\n    request: dict[str, Any], verdict_block: dict[str, Any],\n) -> tuple[ChoiceQuestion, TypedAnswer | ParseFailure]:\n    \"\"\"Parse a typed block against the request with the typed-judgment parser.\"\"\"\n    question = _typed_question_for_request(request)\n    payload = {**verdict_block, \"question_id\": question.question_id, \"primitive\": \"choice\"}\n    return question, parse_single_answer(question, payload)\n\n\ndef stamped_confidence_source(details: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, str | None]:\n    \"\"\"The route's stamp for where the confidence came from, and why it is refused.\n\n    Returns ``(source, error)``. An envelope sealed before the stamp existed\n    is a model's own number: ``self_reported``. ``provider_reported`` stands\n    only when the stamped dispatch model belongs to a provider the fleet\n    marks confidence-native (`model_fleet.provider_reports_confidence`); a\n    spelling the model wrote inside its verdict block is never read.\n    \"\"\"\n    stamp = details.get(\"agent_confidence_source\")\n    if stamp is None:\n        return \"self_reported\", None\n    if stamp not in CONFIDENCE_SOURCES:\n        return \"self_reported\", f\"judge_verdict.confidence_source:invalid:{stamp!r}\"\n    if stamp == \"provider_reported\" and not provider_reports_confidence(details.get(\"agent_dispatch_model\")):\n        return \"self_reported\", \"judge_verdict.confidence_source:unstamped\"\n    return str(stamp), None\n\n\ndef validate_judge_response(\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any],\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Y5 (ORPHAN-706) — the judge output contract, checkable BEFORE accept.\n\n    The second sealed night accepted 12 judge results whose envelopes never\n    carried a verdict the bridge could read (\"expected verdict ..., got\n    None\") — each one an accepted row that could never fold, burning bridge\n    retries toward permanent_fail. This is the SINGLE definition of what a\n    judge response must carry; ``record_judge_verdict_from_response`` raises\n    on exactly this list, and the executor's pre-submit gate releases the\n    claim on it instead of sealing an unfoldable result.\n\n    Returns [] for non-judge roles: the contract binds judges only.\n    \"\"\"\n    role = response.get(\"role\")\n    if not is_judge_role(role):\n        return []\n    errors: list[str] = []\n    details = response.get(\"details\") or {}\n    if not isinstance(details, dict):\n        details = {}\n    verdict_block = _verdict_field(details)\n    if not verdict_block:\n        return [\"judge_verdict:absent\"]\n    _source, source_error = stamped_confidence_source(details)\n    if source_error:\n        errors.append(source_error)\n    typed = typed_verdict_block(verdict_block)\n    if typed is not None:\n        # Typed-judgment plan — the typed law is the parser's: primitive,\n        # value in the closed vocabulary, confidence contract, index+quote\n        # citations against the request's pinned excerpts, rationale.\n        if typed.get(\"primitive\") != \"choice\":\n            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.typed:primitive_mismatch:{typed.get('primitive')!r}\")\n        else:\n            try:\n                _question, outcome = parse_typed_verdict(request, typed)\n            except TypedJudgmentError as exc:\n                outcome = ParseFailure(\"request\", \"evidence_missing\", str(exc))\n            if isinstance(outcome, ParseFailure):\n                errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.typed:{outcome.reason_code}\")\n        verdict = typed.get(\"value\")\n    else:\n        verdict = verdict_block.get(\"verdict\")\n    if verdict not in FEEDBACK_VERDICTS and typed is None:\n        # ORPHAN-CRITICAL-735 — the arbiter's contract says \"return\n        # details.consensus, OR the uncertainty reason when the consensus\n        # gate cannot be met\", and the deterministic engine it mirrors\n        # emits exactly that non-verdict outcome. The live arbiter obeyed\n        # and this contract burned the claim as invalid:None — fourth\n        # mint-vs-law instance. Uncertainty is a VALID arbitration\n        # outcome, for the arbiter alone, with a reason from the closed\n        # vocabulary; binary judges stay bound to binary verdicts.\n        reason = verdict_block.get(\"uncertainty_reason\")\n        if (\n            role == \"consensus_arbitration\"\n            and verdict is None\n            and reason in CONSENSUS_UNCERTAINTY_REASONS\n        ):\n            pass\n        else:\n            errors.append(f\"judge_verdict.verdict:invalid:{verdict!r}\")\n    tool_id = request.get(\"tool_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"tool_id\")\n    run_id = request.get(\"run_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"run_id\")\n    finding_id = request.get(\"finding_id\") or verdict_block.get(\"finding_id\")\n    if not (tool_id and run_id and finding_id):\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_tool_run_or_finding_id\")\n    judge_id = details.get(\"agent_subagent_type\") or verdict_block.get(\"judge_id\")\n    if not judge_id:\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:missing_judge_identity\")\n    elif str(judge_id).startswith(\"ci-executor:\"):\n        errors.append(\"judge_verdict:executor_shaped_judge_identity\")\n    return errors\n\n\ndef record_judge_verdict_from_response(\n    *,\n    request: dict[str, Any],\n    response: dict[str, Any],\n    base_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Translate an ACCEPTED judge response into an ai_judge feedback row.\n\n    Returns the persisted row, or None when the envelope role is not a\n    judge role (the bridge is a no-op for non-judge submissions).\n\n    Raises GovernanceError when the response is missing the data the\n    consensus engine requires (tool_id / run_id / finding_id come from\n    the request envelope's optional fields; verdict + severity from\n    the response.details).\n    \"\"\"\n    role = response.get(\"role\")\n    if not is_judge_role(role):\n        return None","numLines":200,"startLine":60,"totalLines":499}}}

ts-node\nimport { basename, dirname, join, relative, resolve } from 'node:path';\n\nimport ts from 'typescript';\n\nimport {\n  collectFiles,\n  filterFilesBySnapshot,\n  isArchivedWorkspacePath,\n 'high_risk_source_without_adjacent_test'\n  | 'migration_without_test'\n  | 'security_source_without_security_test';\n\ninterface AdapterInput {\n  readonly roots?: readonly string[];\n  readonly readonly path?: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n  readonly name?: string;\n  readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface AdapterFinding {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly rule: const allowlist = new Set((input.allowlist ?? []).map(normalizePath));\n  const files = roots\n    .map((root) => resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, root))\n    .filter((root) => workspacePathExists(root))\n    .flatMap((root) => collectSourceAndTestFiles(root));\n  const snapshotFiles = filterFilesBySnapshot(files, workspaceRoot, input);\n  const units = snapshotFiles.map((file) => readFileUnit(file, workspaceRoot));\n  const tests = units.filter((unit) => unit.isTest);\n  const sources = units.filter((unit) => !unit.isTest);\n  const sourcePaths = new Set(sources.map((source) => source.relativePath));\n  const pathAliases = readPathAliases(workspaceRoot);\n  const result: AnalysisResult = {\n    observations: [],\n    findings: [],\n    readPaths: units.map((unit) => unit.relativePath),\n  };\n\n  for (const test of tests) {\n    result.observations.push({\n      id: `test-gap-test-file:${test.relativePath}`,\n      type: 'test_gap_test_file',\n      path: test.relativePath,\n      line: 1,\n      name: basename(test.relativePath),\n      details: {\n        imports: importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).sort(),\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  let highRiskWithoutTest = 0;\n  let directMatchCount = 0;\n  let weakMatchCount = 0;\n  for (const source of sources) {\n    const risk = classifySourceRisk(source);\n    if (!risk.highRisk && !risk.securitySensitive && !risk.migrationHazard) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    const matchedTests = matchingTests(source, tests, sourcePaths, pathAliases);\n    const weakMatchedTests = weakSymbolTests(source, tests).filter(\n      (test) => !matchedTests.some((matched) => matched.relativePath === test.relativePath),\n    );\n    directMatchCount += matchedTests.length > 0 ? matchingTests(\n  source: FileUnit,\n  tests: readonly FileUnit[],\n  sourcePaths: ReadonlySet<string>,\n  pathAliases: readonly PathAlias[],\n): readonly FileUnit[] {\n  const sourceBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(source.relativePath);\n  const sourceDir = dirname(source.relativePath);\n  return tests.filter((test) => {\n    const testBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(test.relativePath);\n    if (testBase === sourceBase) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    if (dirname(test.relativePath) === sourceDir && testBase.startsWith(sourceBase)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    if (dirname(test.relativePath).endsWith(`${sourceDir}/__tests__`) && testBase.includes(sourceBase)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    return importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).some((specifier) => {\n      const resolved = resolveImportSpecifier(test.relativePath, specifier, sourcePaths, pathAliases);\n      return resolved === source.relativePath;\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction weakSymbolTests(source: FileUnit, tests: readonly FileUnit[]): readonly FileUnit[] {\n  const symbolNames = exportedSymbols(source.sourceFile);\n  if (symbolNames.length === 0) {\n    return [];\n  }\n  return tests.filter((test) => symbolNames.some((symbolName) => symbolName.length > 2 && test.text.includes(symbolName)));\n}\n\nfunction basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(path: string): string {\n  return basename(path)\n    .replace(/\\.(spec|test)\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '')\n    .replace(/\\.(controller|resolver|handler|guard|strategy|middleware|interceptor|service|entity|module|component)\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '')\n    .replace(/\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '');\n}\n\nfunction importedSpecifiers(sourceFile: ts.SourceFile): string[] {\n  const imports: string[] = [];\n  for (const statement of sourceFile.statements) {\n    if (ts.isImportDeclaration(statement) && ts.isStringLiteral(statement.moduleSpecifier)) {\n      imports.push(statement.moduleSpecifier.text);\n    }\n  }\n  return imports;\n}","numLines":50,"startLine":296,"totalLines":494}}}
